近期聯準會官員談話呈現鷹鴿分歧。聯準會理事 Michael Barr 認為，中東局
勢推升能源價格，以及 AI 相關投資熱潮帶動需求增長，均可能阻礙通膨回落至
2%目標 ， 未來仍不排除進一步升息的必要性 ； 惟 紐約聯準銀行總裁 John Williams、
聯準會副主席 Philip Jefferson 及金融監理副主席 Michelle Bowman 則強調，在 9
月升息後，當前並無急迫性立即進一步收緊政策，應先觀察後續通膨、就業及金
融市場數據變化，再評估是否有進一步調整利率的必要。受官員談話轉趨審慎，
以及 9 月非農就業表現疲弱影響，市場對 10 月 FOMC 會議再度升息的預期明顯
降溫，政策焦點逐漸轉向 12 月會議及年底前是否仍有一次升息空間。 
晶圓代工龍頭台積電傳出正評估與馬斯克旗下 Terafab 合作，在美國德州布
局晶圓廠，馬斯克也於社群平台 X 證實雙方正在討論，並表示 「目前僅在討論階
段 ， 但可能會有進展」 。 目前浮上檯面的合作模式包括由台積電持有並營運新廠 ，
SpaceX 或 Terafab 透過入股及承諾採購晶圓參與；另一方案則由 Terafab 掌握多
數股權 ， 台積電提供製程技術與晶圓廠營運經驗 。Terafab 旨在因應 Tesla、SpaceX
及 xAI 快速成長的 AI 晶片需求，首期投資規模達 168 億美元。若合作成真，不
僅可望成為台積電繼亞利桑那州後在美國的新布局，也凸顯先進製程與穩定晶片
供應的重要性，但目前具體合作方式、時程與投資內容仍未正式定案。 
二、歐元區—法國殖利率大幅上升，不利於投資及消費表現 
 歐元區 9 月 HICP 通膨 、 核心通膨(不含食品及能源)分別上升 0.6、0.1 個百分
點至 3.8%、2.5%， 主因國際原油價格上漲 ， 使能源通膨上升4.5 個百分點至 18.8%，
並透過肥料、物流成本上漲，間接推升食品通膨 0.3 個百分點至 1.4%。另外，服
務通膨上升 0.2 個百分點至 3.2%，近三個月服務通膨大致呈現橫向波動，部分反
映旅遊旺季因素影響。基於布蘭特原油持續位於每桶 90 美元以上，預期年底前
仍將對通膨帶來額外壓力，後續須關注能源漲價可能會透過運輸、物流等成本管
道傳導至核心商品及服務價格，進而提高核心通膨黏性。 
 其他經濟數據包含 ：(1)歐元區 8 月失業率持平 6.4%， 失業人數增加2.6 萬人 ，
主要是義大利勞動參與人數增加所致，不過 ，基於PMI 顯示近期德國勞動雇用需
求持續增溫，將有助於穩定勞動市場表現；(2)歐元區第二季家庭儲蓄率下降 0.2
個百分點至 14.2%，顯示通膨壓力削弱民眾購買力，致儲蓄暫時性減少；(3)歐元
區第二季房價年增率為 4%， 較第一季降0.6 個百分點 ， 主因法國中古屋面臨嚴格

 近一週法國 10 年期公債殖利率升至 4.9%以上，與德國公債殖利率差距擴大
至 125 個基點以上，主要反映市場對法國主權公債可持續性疑慮升溫。儘管目前
政府計畫將 2027 年財政赤字縮限至 5%，惟政府在國會並無穩定多數席次，即使
能以行政命令強行通過預算 ， 也可能面臨倒閣風險 ；此外 ，市場對於2027 年 4 月
總統大選後，新總統能否取得國會足夠支持，推動削減赤字與控制債務的改革亦
缺乏信心。因此未來法國仍可能面臨殖利率進一步上升與財政壓力加劇的風險，
暫時不利於投資及消費信心。 
三、日本—景氣溫和復甦，但消費放緩與通膨升溫風險升高 
 8 月工業生產月減 1.7%，主要係受到該月地震及豪雨等天然災害影響，導致
部分工廠暫時停產。從細項來看，汽車業受停工影響最為明顯，產量下滑6.8%，
電子產品產量亦減少 1.9%。 惟生產機械產量連續三個月成長 ， 加上核心資本財出
貨持續增加，顯示企業投資需求仍相當強勁，有助於支撐後續工業生產回升。從
企業預期來看，製造商預期9 月產量將成長 3.2%，顯示生產活動可望迅速回溫，
在供應鏈逐步恢復、庫存回補需求持續，以及穩健的企業資本支出支撐下，預計
第三季工業生產仍可維持正成長，且成長動能可望延續至年底。 
 8 月零售月減 1.2%，雖然表現略優於市場預期，但仍顯示民間消費動能正在
放緩。此次疲弱主要原因來自耐久財銷售下滑，其中家電銷售因空調等產品在換
機需求告一段落後明顯回落，汽車銷售也隨政府刺激措施效應逐漸消退而連續第
四個月下降。相較之下，食品、服裝等非耐久財銷售雖同步走弱，但降幅相對有
限 ， 反映薪資成長仍對消費提供部分支撐 。惟不利天候與通膨壓力仍是主要風險 ，
8 月至 9 月持續豪雨已影響服裝等非必需消費，而除米價以外的食品及商品價格
持續上漲，也正侵蝕家庭實質購買力，恐限制消費者信心與消費復甦力道。 
 9 月東京核心通膨年增率升至 2.7%，雖然東京都免費托兒及免費水費政策造
成的基期效應貢獻約 0.5 個百分點，但通膨升溫主因仍是價格上漲已由能源與食
品擴散至整體經濟。受石油價格上揚帶動，企業開始加速將原物料及薪資成本轉
嫁給消費者，帶動商品與服務價格普遍走高；租金通膨更升至 2%，創 1994 年以
來新高，公共交通及醫療等項目也開始調漲。隨著東京的物價壓力逐步反映至全
國數據，日本央行進一步升息的可能性升高。

四、中國—為刺激內需、引導產業走向，人行漸近式放寬政策工具 
9 月官方製造業 PMI 上升 0.3 點至 50.1，為近三個月高點，新訂單、出口訂
單指數分別為 50.5、50，並帶動生產指數上升至 51.7，不過，庫存指數下降、就
業指數加速衰退、企業展望減弱，皆拖累 PMI 指數水準。此外，投入、產出物價
指數分別上升至 60.8、54，顯示成本壓力加劇，但售價調整速度依然較為遲緩，
不利於獲利表現。官方非製造業 PMI 上升 1.2 點至 50.2，其中建築業活動指數大
增 3.4 點至 50.3，脫離連續八個月的衰退區間，主要是政府加大財政刺激力度，
推動重大建設開工；非製造業的從業人員指數為 45.9，顯示就業情況仍顯嚴峻，
亦較不利於內需表現。 
鑒於內需動能疲弱，人行於 9 月 29 日宣布下調抵押補充貸款(該貸款本質是
將資金引導至政府希望發展的領域)利率自 1.75%降至 1.50%，並擴大適用範圍，
將水網、新型電網、算力網、新一代通信網、城市地下管網及物流網等建設納入
支持，有助於提振內需動能。未來若內需復甦力道不如預期，不排除人行將進一
步降準或擴大結構性貨幣工具支持經濟；至於全面降息則仍須兼顧人民幣匯率及
中美利差等因素，可能性相對較低。 
五、印度及東南亞—泰國、菲律賓出口均達 2 成以上的高速成長

2026 獲利亮眼且擁高現金殖利率

永豐觀點 
2026 年獲利逆勢上揚且淡季運價緩跌有撐，擁8%現金殖利率。觀察12 月巴拿馬
運河水位。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：(1) 美洲持續暢旺到明年春節，亞洲進入旺季，運價淡季相對有撐，(2) 
2027 年新增運力被碼頭作業能量不易擴充而抵銷，實質有效運力可能低於市場預
期，(3) 高現金殖利率的保護，下檔有撐，維持買進，目標價由 256 元(0.9X2026 
BVPS)上調到 278 元(0.85X2027BVPS)。

均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。

長榮為全球第 7 大海運業者：長榮海運是全球第 7 大航商，6 月底集團運力 204
萬 TEU，242 艘船舶，市佔率 5.9%，2025 年底運力 195.6 萬 TEU(+11%YoY)，
2026-2027 運力分別為 207.1 萬 TEU(+5.9%YoY)和 241 萬 TEU(+15.9%YoY)，
待交船 72 艘約 93.5 萬 TEU。1H26 年運送 545 萬 TEU(+4.2%YoY)，平均運費
1034 美元/TEU(-7%YoY)，2025 年航線別美洲線佔 39%，歐洲線佔 28%、亞洲
線佔 18%，其他佔 15%。 
3Q 運價強勢 ，4Q 運價相對有撐 ：1H26 美洲線艙位成長 0.96%， 貨量成長5.1%，
歐洲線艙位成長 7.5%，貨量成長 12.2%。地緣政治風險導致供應鏈不穩旺季提前
運送而上漲， 亞洲重要港口碼頭使用率偏高， 天候因素容易導致碼頭作業效率低造
成塞港與巴拿馬運河水位降低等均影響有效運力，支撐美東線運價重回 1 萬美元
/FEU 關卡，10/8 巴拿馬運河水位 85.2 英尺(-1.93%YoY)較 9 月中略有回升，但
11 月底水位雖回升到 85.9 英尺但低於去年同期 3.4%，航商預期美洲線貨量持續
暢旺到農曆春節，觀察 2Q27 美國是否啟動升息循環影響貨量。

2026 年供過增長率最低的一年：Alphaliner 預估 2026 需求成長 2.5%， 供給成長
率 4.7%，2026 年船舶供給成長率是近幾年最低的，目前貨櫃船在手訂單1400 萬
TEU，未來三年面臨新船大量下水壓力，然而公司認為受到地緣政治影響船期不穩
定性高、碼頭使用率偏高易受突發事件導致塞港、2028 年美西港口工人合約到期
等均影響有效運力，即使紅海復航採逐班逐航線，影響也會被抵銷，因此2027 年
的名目供給能否 100%轉為實質運力有待考驗 。 目前全球運力因為碼頭作業能量受
限、氣候因素、繞道、降速等影響 11%運力。 
美國零售進口 2026 年預計成長 1%：根據美國零售協會預估，2026 年 1-7 月貨
櫃進口 14.97 百萬 TEU(+0.3%YoY) ， 9 月進口量達到高峰 2.31 百萬
TEU(+10%YoY)，10 月開始下滑，但仍保持年成長，2026 年預估進口 25.7 百萬
TEU(+1%YoY)。

Document 107: 261020_ml_nienmade

### Page 1

Nien Made Enterprise

3Q preview: EPS +13% YoY to NT$6.6; 
Better 2H but not yet a turnaround

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 441.00 TWD | Price: 358.50 TWD

3Qe EPS NT$6.6: strong rev and GP offset by opex and FX 
Nien Made is scheduled to report 3Q26 results on 4 Nov. Disclosed monthly sales imply 
3Q26 revenue growth of 16% YoY to NT$8.5bn, or 9% in USD terms, largely 
volume-driven. Growth reaccelerated from 1.7% YoY in 1H26, led by custom-made on a 
low US base and expansion in Europe and Japan. Ready-made also improved as 
Cambodia labor shortages eased. (See Exhibit 1 for US window coverings spending 
trends). Gross margin: We expect underlying GPM to rise 1.1ppt YoY to 59.8% on lower 
tariffs, broadly flat QoQ. We forecasts an NT$300mn of IEEPA tariff refunds in 2H26. 
Assuming NT$150mn is booked in 3Q, this would add 1.7ppt and lift reported GPM to 
61.5%. We expect the selling expense ratio to fall 1.7ppt QoQ to 17.5% on lower fuel 
charges, but remain 0.5ppt higher YoY due to continued salesforce and distribution 
network investment. We expect an FX loss in 3Q26 vs. an NT$118mn gain in 3Q25, 
weighing on YoY earnings growth. We forecast 3Q26 EPS of NT$6.6, up 13% YoY. 
US window covering picking up, but no turnaround yet  
BofA credit/debit card data suggests US spending on window coverings turned positive 
in Aug-26 for the first time after 19 consecutive months of YoY declines. Spending rose 
4%/3% YoY in Aug/Sep, respectively. This improvement partly explains the recent 
reacceleration in Nien Made's monthly sales. That said, we view the improvement more 
as normalization off a low base than the start of an industry turnaround. The Aug-Sep 
2025 comparison base was relatively weak, while the current rate-hike environment 
remains a headwind to housing-related demand. 
Slightly raise FY26 EPS est. to NT$22.9; PO to NT$441 
We raise our FY26 EPS estimate by 2% to NT$22.9 to reflect the blended impact of 
stronger-than-expected revenue growth and FX loss. We maintain our FY26 DPS forecast 
of NT$17 per share. We raise our PO by 4% to NT$441, as we roll forward our valuation 
basis from FY26E EPS to FY27E EPS. At the same time, we lower our target P/E from 
19x to 17x, reflecting the MSCI removal and a less supportive rate environment.

* For full definitions of iQmethod SM measures, see page 7.

Company Sector 
Interior Furnishings

Company Description 
Nien Made, founded in 1974, is one of the world's largest 
window-covering manufacturers. It has roughly 50% 
market share in both ready-made (OEM business) shades 
and blinds, and custom-made (own brand) shutters in the 
US. The US market accounted for over 75% of sales. Key 
clients include Home Depot and Wal-Mart for ready-made 
shades and blinds. Nien Made operates its own retail 
network and e-commerce business for its custom-made 
shutters under the Norman brand name in the US and 
Europe.

Investment Rationale 
We have Buy rating on Nien Made, mainly given 1) clear 
custom-made growth profile (share gain in the US and fast-
growing DTC in Japan and Europe), 2) its largest customer 
Home Depot is gaining market share, and 3) margin 
expansion potentials backed by favorable products mix.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Nien Made Enterprise (XNRBF) 
Our PO NT$441 is derived from 17x P/E 2027E EPS. This is around 0.5SD above 
historical the one-year forward P/E, which we think is warranted given the visibility of 
the growth profile.

Downside risks are 1) weaker-than-expected Home Depot's sell-through, 2) slow 
recovery of US housing turnover, and 3) higher tariff increases on China exports to the 
US. 4) Loss of key customers.

Business Performance Numerator Denominator

Amortization 
Return On Equity Net Income Shareholders’ Equity 
Operating Margin Operating Profit Sales 
Earnings Growth Expected 5 Year CAGR From Latest Actual N/A 
Free Cash Flow Cash Flow From Operations − Total Capex N/A

Cash Realization Ratio Cash Flow From Operations Net Income 
Asset Replacement Ratio Capex Depreciation 
Tax Rate Tax Charge Pre-Tax Income 
Net Debt-To-Equity Ratio Net Debt = Total Debt − Cash & Equivalents Total Equity 
Interest Cover EBIT Interest Expense  
Valuation Toolkit Numerator Denominator 
Price / Earnings Ratio Current Share Price Diluted Earnings Per Share (Basis As Specified) 
Price / Book Value Current Share Price Shareholders’ Equity / Current Basic Shares 
Dividend Yield Annualised Declared Cash Dividend Current Share Price 
Free Cash Flow Yield Cash Flow From Operations − Total Capex Market Cap = Current Share Price × Current Basic Shares 
Enterprise Value / Sales EV = Current Share Price × Current Shares + Minority Equity + Net Debt +

iQmethod are: A consistently structured, detailed, and transparent methodology. Guidelines to maximize the effectiveness of the comparative valuation process, and to identify some common pitfalls. 
iQdatabase ®  is our real-time global research database that is sourced directly from our equity analysts’ earnings models and includes forecasted as well as historical data for income statements, balance sheets, and cash 
flow statements for companies covered by BofA Global Research.

Issue & News 
SK하이닉스  
(000660) 
 소음보다 방향성에 
류형근

hyungkeun.ryu@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 3,200,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.09.23) 
1,862,000 
반도체업종

◼ 최근 시장은 2가지 Risk를 제기. 다만, 과도한 우려는 기우. 
◼ 현 주가는 보다 강인해지는 Fundamental의 가치를 미반영한 수준. 
◼ 비중 확대 전략 유효. 목표주가와 매수의견 유지.

투자전략: 업황을 대변하지 못하고 있는 주가 
목표주가와 매수의견 유지. 현 주가는 업황의 강세를 충분히 반영하지 못하
고 있다고 생각. 1) 메모리반도체 사이클의 추가 확장 (구매 경쟁 심화에 따른 
단기 가격 상승 폭 확대, 기대치를 상회할 4Q26 DRAM 판매량), 2) 3Q26 실
적발표에서 보다 구체화될 주주환원 정책의 방향성에 보다 집중할 필요. 
시장에서 제기하는 Risk가 있다면, 2가지. 1) 주요 고객사향 HBM4 품질 이슈, 
2) Solidigm IPO와 해외 공장 투자에 따른 경계심리 확대가 그 대상. 당사는 
해당 Risk가 Fundamental을 저해할 것으로 보고 있지 않으며, 단기 노이즈보
다는 사이클 확장의 선순환에 보다 주목할 필요가 있다고 생각. 
1. HBM4 품질 이슈, 과도한 우려는 기우 
3Q26부터 HBM4 출하가 본격화될 것이라는 기존 입장을 유지. HBM4 비중
은 3Q26 전체 판매량의 40%, 2027년 연간 50% 중반까지 상승할 것. 
일각에서는 주요 고객사향 HBM4 품질 이슈로 사업 성과가 기대치를 하회할 
수 있다는 우려를 표출. 다만, 과도한 우려는 기우. 후공정 단에서 문제가 일
부 발생했을 수 있으나, 해결 불가능한 Risk라 보기 어렵고, 불량에 따른 배
상금을 논할 단계도 아니라는 판단. 
2. 해외 공장 투자, 실보다 득이 크다 
언론보도에 따르면, SK하이닉스는 해외 공장 투자와 Solidigm IPO를 추진 중. 
해당 결정이 현실화될 경우, 시장은 단기적으로 이를 부정적으로 받아들일 
가능성이 존재. 다만, 결과적으로, 실보다 득이 큰 선택이 될 것이라 생각. 
- 해외 투자가 현실화되더라도, 실질 생산 기여 시점은 2028년 이후일 가능
성이 높음. 미국의 건설 경기와 공장 구축의 세부 단계 등을 감안할 필요. 
- 해외 투자 결정이 관세 Risk를 축소시킬 수 있는 변화라는 점도 감안할 필
요. “불확실성 해소”는 Upcycle의 중반부에서 반도체 주식에 대한 좋은 투자
포인트가 되어왔고, 이번에도 다르지 않을 것이라는 판단.

Document 219: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로긴 호흡으로 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로긴 호흡으로 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

(005930) 
삼성전자 
2026.09.29

반도체 손인준 
6154 / sij@eugenefn.com

긴 호흡으로 
 투자의견 STRONG BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 560,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 270,000원(9/28)

 삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익이 103.6조원(+16%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. 메모리 가격 등 전반적인 반도체 업황에 대한 기존 가정은 대체로 
유지했으나, 최근 원화 강세에 따라 원/달러 환율 가정을 1,440원에서 
1,400원으로 낮추고 MX 사업부의 적자 확대를 반영해 실적 추정치를 하
향 조정했다. 또한 2Q26 재고가 3Q26 매출로 인식되는 과정에서 발생하
는 추가 성과급 충당금 부담도 반영했다.  
 DS 부문의 3Q26 영업이익은 104.0조원(+17%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. 서버 DRAM과 eSSD 중심의 가격 인상이 이어지며, DRAM과 
NAND의 Blended ASP는 각각 +17%, +18%QoQ 상승할 것으로 추정한
다. 1cnm 수율 개선과 HBM4의 빠른 램프업도 실적 개선에 기여할 전망이
다. Foundry/LSI는 HBM4 베이스다이 생산 확대, Groq 3 LPU 양산 및 선
단 공정 가격 인상에 힘입어 하반기 매출 성장이 예상된다. 다만 성과급 충
당금과 감가상각비 부담으로 영업적자 축소 폭은 제한적일 것으로 보인다. 
 DX 부문의 3Q26 영업이익은 -1.8조원(적자지속qoq)을 기록할 것으로 추
정한다. 삼성전자는 메모리 가격 급등에도 제품 가격 인상을 제한하며 시장 
점유율 확대에 주력하고 있다. 이러한 가격 정책과 갤럭시 Z 폴드8 등 신제
품 흥행에 힘입어 판매량은 당초 예상치를 상회할 것으로 보인다. 다만 원
가 상승분의 전가가 제한되면서 영업적자 폭은 크게 확대될 전망이다.  
 삼성전자의 4Q26 영업이익은 114.9조원(+11%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. DRAM과 NAND의 Blended ASP는 각각 +11%, +12%QoQ 상승
하며 견조한 흐름을 이어갈 것으로 보인다. 환율 가정 조정에 따라 2026년
과 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 각각 365.2조원(+735%yoy), 595.8조원
(+63%YoY)으로 낮췄다. 4Q26~1Q27 범용 DRAM·NAND 및 2027년 
HBM 가격 협상 환경은 공급업체에 유리하게 전개되고 있어, 향후 가격 가
정의 추가 상향 가능성이 높다고 판단한다.  
 극심한 DRAM 공급 부족으로 인한 HBM 적층 단수 하향 조정(base die 수
요 증가), Memory Wall에 대응하기 위한 3D Stacked DRAM·zHBM 도입 
논의 등은 파운드리의 기술적 가치를 부각시키는 요인이다. 파운드리의 수
익성 회복 속도는 완만할 전망이지만, 메모리와 파운드리를 연계한 턴키 비
즈니스의 시너지는 AI 반도체 시장 내 경쟁력을 높이고, 메모리 시장 점유
율 확대에도 기여할 것으로 보인다. 적극적인 메모리 가격 인상 정책으로 
업계 최고 수준의 Blended ASP도 장기간 유지할 것으로 예상한다. 
 이제 메모리 업종 주가는 업황의 지속 가능성을 확인하고 꾸준한 주주환원
을 반영하며, 중장기적으로 점진적인 상승세를 이어갈 것으로 판단한다. 삼
성전자는 메모리·파운드리를 아우르는 턴키 역량과 가격 정책에 기반한 실
적 차별화를 통해 피어 그룹 내 경쟁력을 높여갈 것으로 보인다.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

Document 278: Bank of Japan hits 2% price target

Source PDF: all report pdf/Bank of Japan hits 2% price target.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

In a big day for monetary policy in Japan, the Bank of Japan
ﬁnally announced it had hit its 2% price target – a target set in
2013. The question now for markets is how quickly the BoJ
takes the policy rate to neutral. We now think it will hike in
December and March. A hawkish BoJ can help keep a lid on
USD/JPY at 160
Chris Turner
The Bank of Japan in Tokyo
CPI is consistent with price stability target
In its October outlook report released today, the Bank of Japan

ﬁnally got to say that "underlying CPI inﬂation is at a level that is
generally consistent with the price stability target of 2%, and is
expected to remain at around that level." That was a target set in
2013 and one the BoJ has battled to hit after decades of deﬂation.
In terms of what that means for monetary policy from here, the
clear message is to expect further tightening. The debate now

### Page 2

shifts to how quickly the BoJ takes rates to neutral, seen in the
1.75-2.00% area.
For today, the policy rate was left at 1.25%, as back-to-back hikes
could have upset markets. But running through the new Outlook
Report were three themes that the BoJ felt could all contribute to
upside price risks: energy prices, AI demand and the weak yen. In
that report, growth forecasts were left around 0.7-0.8% (near or
above potential), while CPI forecasts were revised slightly higher.

There remained several references to labour shortages and a
conﬁdence that strong corporate proﬁts would be feeding into
wages and higher inﬂation. There was also an acknowledgement
that interest rates were rising overseas and presumably a fear that
a failure to track Federal Reserve and European Central Bank rate
hikes in December could leave the yen vulnerable again.
We now think the BoJ will hike rates by 25bp in both December and
March and will probably be comfortable with a policy rate around

1.75% ahead of further government measures to support
consumption next April.
What was slightly surprising was the BoJ announcing an
acceleration in its ETF sales as it tries to unwind its balance sheet
and improve market functioning. After running at JPY330bn per
year since 2025, ETF sales will be accelerated to JPY860bn per year
starting in 2027. This looks to be a sign of conﬁdence in the
Japanese economy from the BoJ.
BoJ's latest growth and inﬂation forecasts

Source: Bank of Japan
Market reaction
USD/JPY was slightly softer amid a broadly weaker dollar today.
The market already prices the BoJ policy rate being taken to 2.00%
next summer, meaning that today’s hawkish BoJ communication

was no surprise. 10-year Japanese government bond yields have
matched the drop in US Treasury yields this month, and their
outperformance of Treasuries since July must be welcome news to
policymakers in Tokyo.
Barring the US midterms or a sizeable AI correction taking a big
chunk out of the dollar, we expect USD/JPY to stay bid towards the
upper end of a 155-160 range over the coming months. We do not
see USD/JPY turning decisively lower until the second quarter next

year, when we look for the dollar to turn.
The surprise package here could be that USD/JPY does a USD/KRW –
where AI demand ﬁres up broader domestic demand growth in
Japan, conﬁdence grows in the domestic economy, and more
portfolio and trade ﬂows are brought home. One to watch in 2027.

Document 304: De-reservification, Not De-dollarization (2026-09-15)

Source PDF: all report pdf/De-reservification, Not De-dollarization (2026-09-15).pdf
Report source: CFR (Follow the Money)
Date: 2026-09-15
Pages: 17
Status: ok

### Page 1

1
EXPERT TAKE FOLLOW THE MONEY
De-reservification, Not De-dollarization
The world isn’t moving away from the dollar. It is shifting its dollars from traditional
reserves to state banks, pension funds, and other quasi-sovereign investors.
(Bernardo Montoya/Courtesy Reuters)
PUBLISHED
September 15, 2026 8:37 a.m.
Articles Follow the Money Share
Log In
Regions Topics Research Experts Events The Future of American Strategy

### Page 2

2
Brad W. Setser CFR EXPERT
Whitney Shepardson Senior Fellow
This is a joint blog with Alex Etra, Senior Macro
Strategist and head of Flow of Funds at Vanda
Research.
The IMF’s data on the currency composition of
foreign exchange (FX) reserves is scrutinized carefully
for any signs that the world is shifting way from the
dollar.
Yet it doesn’t really matter that much if the dollar’s
share of reserves has shifted from 56.5 percent to 57
percent. The ink-to-impact ratio of the quarterly

reporting on the latest COFER data is all off.
Neither the stock of global reserves nor the stock of
dollar reserves has changed much in the last ten years.
The action is elsewhere.

### Page 3

3
China’s state banks (per the Bank of International
Settlements) have almost as many foreign assets as the
central bank (PBOC).
Japan’s Government Pension Investment Fund
(GPIF) has almost as many foreign assets ($986
billion) as the government has FX reserves ($1.1
trillion at the end of August). Japan’s FX reserves are
on the books of the Ministry of Finance and the
GPIF is overseen by the Ministry of Health, Welfare
and Labor—so these are almost all assets of the

Government of Japan or the broader public sector,
not its central bank.
Korea’s National Pension Service has more foreign
assets than the Bank of Korea has FX reserves.
A different dynamic is in play in Taiwan, as the
government doesn’t run a big retirement fund. But in

### Page 4

4
a world where the hedge ratio of the life insurers has
been (and still is) used as a policy tool, the shift in
Taiwan’s foreign asset accumulation away from the
Central Bank of China (CBC) and over to
institutions whose activities in the foreign currency
market it directly influences broadly fits into the same
theme. So too does the PBOC’s use of the
“macroprudential adjustment factor” effectively adjust
banks’ net foreign assets in much the same way that

CBC regulations adjust lifer hedge ratios.
In the last year, the lifers’ hedge ratios—and their
direct hedges with the central bank—have been one
of the key tools the CBC has used to manage the
Taiwan dollar (and guard the lifers’ solvency).
Asia’s big surplus economies are in a sense just
catching up with the world’s oil exporters. Norway
put its oil surplus into a sovereign wealth fund from
the start. Kuwait, Qatar, and Abu Dhabi (the most
oil-rich emirate) all also have large sovereign funds.

And the world’s biggest oil exporter has joined them:
The Public Investment Fund has almost as many
foreign assets as the Saudi Central Bank (SAMA).*
What’s more, the available evidence suggests that the
dollar share of these pools is in line with or higher
than the dollar share of global FX reserves (it depends
on the institution and jurisdiction).

### Page 5

5
In other words, by looking at the (easily available)
data on the world’s static holdings of formal FX
reserve assets, scholars and analysts miss most of the
growth in the world’s sovereign and quasi-sovereign
foreign assets.
Consider China.
No serious analyst now disputes that China’s state
banks—including the policy banks (the China
Development Bank, China Exim)—hold several
trillion in foreign assets.
China reports $3.3 trillion in gross foreign assets to

the BIS (the net position, counting foreign bank
claims on the entire Chinese economy not just the
banks, is $2.5 trillion). That maps to the BOP data,
which shows almost $4 trillion in gross outflows
through the banking system (technically, the sum of
gross outflows in “other” plus the $500 billion in
foreign currency bonds held by the state commercial
banks)

### Page 6

6
Since 2010 (and even more so after 2014) the net
outflow through the state banks has exceeded reserve
accumulation.
The broad contours of this story are confirmed by the
balance sheet data reported by state commercial
banks in their 2025 annual reports, which showed

### Page 7

7
that the top five banks held a combined $2.5 trillion
in foreign currency assets (mostly held abroad).
The Chinese haven’t disclosed the foreign assets of
the two policy banks (with at this stage the complicity
of the IMF, which has neither analyzed the role of
SAFE policy bank financing, nor highlighted the
glaring gap in China’s own reporting). But the work
of AidData points to nearly $1 trillion in foreign
assets, with a hefty dollar share.
The available data sources all suggest that the bulk of

banks—puts the dollar share of their offshore foreign
currency assets at around 70 percent. That is well
above the last disclosed dollar share of China’s formal
reserves (55 percent in 2019). The banks’ annual

### Page 8

8
reports suggest that about 70 percent of the banks’
assets are in dollars and Hong Kong dollars (55
percent USD/15 percent HKD)—a bit below the
dollar and Hong Kong dollar share of their liabilities
(swaps out of the HKD fund a decent share of the
banks euro assets).
Put simply, SAFE’s static dollar holdings aren’t the
important story.
The real story is the rapid growth of the state banks
dollar holdings.
Japan holds a high (though undisclosed) dollar share

in its FX reserves, and those reserves are primarily
invested in Treasuries—so Japan’s MoF is now clearly
the largest contributor to the U.S. data on foreign
official holdings of Treasuries. (SAFE has shifted its
funds out of U.S. custodians, and thus increasingly
appears in the data as a “private” holder in a European
custodial center)
Japan’s formal reserves haven’t changed much in the
last ten years; periodic sales to limit the yen’s weakness
have offset most of the MoF’s accumulated interest

income. Foreign currency reserves were $1.2 trillion
in 2014 and $1.16 trillion at the end of Q1. They
dipped to about $1 trillion at the end of August after
sales of close to $100 billion at the end of July/start of
August.

### Page 9

9
What has grown is the foreign portfolio of the GPIF.
it was around $400 billion in 2014, ~$1 trillion in Q1
of 2026.
GPIF’s foreign bonds aren’t held exclusively in dollars,
but roughly 52 percent of its foreign bonds (26
percent of all bonds) are USD-denominated.
But the GPIF, like most international investors, holds
a large share of its equity portfolio in the U.S. (~$165
billion, 65 percent of the foreign equity portfolio).
Korea is a more extreme version of Japan.

The Bank of Korea’s FX reserves have been roughly
$375-450 billion over the last ten years or so. Around
two-thirds of those are now in dollars (as of the end of
2025).

### Page 10

10
From 2012 to 2025, the foreign assets at the NPS
have grown from ~$80 billion (around 21 percent of
total assets) to over $660 billion (55 percent of total
assets)—and something like 70 percent of those are in
dollars. 76 percent of the fund’s equities are in dollars,
and 66 percent of its debt. And while a lower share of
its “alternative” portfolio is in North America, most
alternative mandates are awarded in dollars.

### Page 11

11
In other words, the majority of the dollar assets held
by Korea’s government (the NPS reports to the
Ministry of Health and Welfare, and the Minister of
Health chairs its asset allocation committee) aren’t
held by the central bank and aren’t in the IMF’s
COFER data set.
Taiwan is an unusual case, as the life insurers aren’t
technically sovereign investors. But with their
regulator now explicitly encouraging the them to hold
an unhedged foreign currency book and hence they

now rely on the state for their solvency, they have a bit
of an official character (a significant appreciation of
the TWD would blow through their volatility reserves
and leave them with fewer assets than liabilities, as
their foreign assets are largely held against TWD
policies). The CBC certainly now uses the lifers desire
to unwind existing hedges to help manage the Taiwan

### Page 12

12
dollar. And they have an exceptionally high dollar
share in their foreign assets—over 95 percent.
The Saudi Public Investment Fund (PIF) is another
example.
It keeps roughly $200 billion of its $250 billion in
foreign investments in dollars—well above the U.S.
share of a standard global stock index. Almost all of
the PIF’s $30 billion in external debt is also in dollars.
And the PIF now has only slightly fewer foreign
assets than the Saudi Central Bank has reserves.**

The broad story is thus pretty clear: the growth in the
world’s sovereign and quasi-sovereign assets is not
coming through an increase in FX reserve holdings
managed by the world’s central banks.

### Page 13

13
And, in the critical case of China, the dollar share of
China’s quasi-sovereign foreign assets is likely higher
than the dollar share of their formal FX reserves.
Adam Tooze has noted that inflows into the U.S. are
now coming from investors looking for a profit, not
investors looking for safety. What he calls the “profit
dollar” manifests itself in a number of different ways.
More inflows into corporate bonds and equities for
one. Less official demand for Treasuries for another.

More opacity too.
Those same trends are at play among sovereign and
quasi-sovereign investors.
So don’t obsess about the dollar’s chare in formal FX
reserves. Do recognize that the dollar’s “reserve
currency role” isn’t the source of any significant new
inflows into the dollar.

### Page 14

14
The dollar’s dominant role in the international
monetary system depends on much more than the
size and composition of central bank FX reserves. It is
as much, perhaps more so (given that FX reserve
managers are themselves ultimately liability matchers)
a function of the portfolio choice of a set of
private/semi-private/quasi-sovereign investors that
are much more difficult to observe. Reserve currency
status is not only about FX reserves in a strict sense.

That’s been true for some time. Most of the current
flow into U.S. from the “official” sector is coming
from investors who are not classic reserve managers.
Those flows remain heavily tilted toward the dollar, at
least for now.***
And any real de-dollarization would likely occur first
among these investors.
Put differently, the dollar’s global role is increasingly
as a source of returns, not a source of safety. The
foreign bid, private and public, is for risk, not for

Treasuries. That doesn’t help Scott Bessent much
right now, but it has helped keep valuations in the
stock market extended. And the global debate on the
dollar’s role lags the evolution in the dollar’s role, and
the risks associated with that new role.
It is currently fashionable in some circles to point to
the diminution of the “convenience yield” on

### Page 15

15
Treasuries even while the U.S. dollar continues to
enjoy such a privilege. But the shift in the global
investor base, and their apparent portfolio preference
for U.S. risk assets in lieu of Treasuries, may help
explain the divergence between the convenience yield
on UST and the convenience yield on USD.
And of course, if the negative convenience yield
implies that Treasuries are trading at a historical
discount, the persistence of the latter—and its

concentration in risk assets—suggests that the “profit
dollar” itself is likely trading at a historical premium.
That apparent premium on U.S. risk assets deserves as
much (or more) scrutiny as the currency composition
of central bank FX reserves.
* The PIF and Saudi Aramco also now have a certain
amount of debt.
** Reserves (now mostly in Treasuries, so should
appear in the TIC data, unlike at some times in the
past.

### Page 16

16

Council on Foreign Relations is an independent,
nonpartisan membership organization, think tank, and
publisher, and takes no institutional positions on matters
of policy.

share is below the likely dollar share of Japan’s MoF.

Document 322: Financial conditions in the Eurozone_ a tightening temporarily tempered by credit volumes (2026-10-07)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Financial conditions in the Eurozone_ a tightening temporarily tempered by credit volumes (2026-10-07).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

7 October 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
FINANCIAL CONDITIONS IN THE EUROZONE: A TIGHTENING TEMPORARILY TEMPERED BY CREDIT VOLUMES
Thomas Humblot
*LATEST OBSERVATION: 5 OCTOBER 2026  **ESTIMATE
Since June 2026, the ECB has been publishing 1 a new indicator that helps to assess the transmission of its monetary policy to the economy:
the Broad Intermediation Gauge 2 (ECB-BIG). This new tool has revealed that, thus far, strong credit growth has limited the restrictive impact

of tightening financial conditions caused by rising interest rates in the Eurozone. However, this offsetting effect is beginning to fade.
The ECB-BIG: a new indicator for a broader view of financial conditions
The ECB-BIG complements the range of indicators used by the European Central Bank (ECB) to assess the effective transmission of its monetary
policy. This new tool provides a comprehensive view of how restrictive (or expansive) financial conditions are in the Eurozone. It stands out

for its approach that focuses more specifically on intermediation conditions. Therefore, the ECB-BIG provides a more comprehensive view than
most other financial condition indicators of financial conditions. In fact, it covers variables relating to the volume of bank lending to households
and businesses (the ‘volume’ component). Traditionally, this type of indicator tends to focus more on market variables (primarily interest and
exchange rates), share prices and corporate spreads. In addition, the ‘price’ component of the ECB-BIG is extended to include the cost of bank

loans, which is another new feature.
The rise in interest rates was temporarily offset by increased lending
By aggregating the variables relating to banking and non-banking intermediation conditions, the ECB-BIG shows that rising interest rates
(negative price effect) were offset in particular by lending volumes, which have continued to rise in 2026 (positive volume effect). A more
‘traditional’ indicator of financial conditions, the Macro-Finance FCI (MF-FCI) — which is, incidentally, included in the ECB-BIG — reveals a

much more pronounced tightening over the same period (see the orange curve in the chart). However, this offsetting of the price effect by the
volume effect is now beginning to wane, a sign that monetary policy is being effectively transmitted to the economy. Financial intermediaries
are gradually passing on the rise in rates to their customers (see the dotted green lines, which represent the composite indicators of the cost
of new loans to households and businesses, combining interest rates for different maturities), while lending volumes are falling. As a result,

between July and August 2026, seasonally adjusted flows of loans to households and businesses almost halved.
1 P . Lane, 2026, The Outlook for the Euro Area Economy, Background slides for Reuters NEXT Europe, London, 16 June 2026
2 A. Allayioti et al., 2026, The ECB-BIG index: tracking credit conditions in the euro area, ECB Economic Bulletin, Issue 5/2026
SOURCE: ALLAYIOTI AND AL. (2026), ECB, BLETZINGER ET AL. (2026), BNP PARIBAS ESTIMATES

In the longer term, financial conditions are expected to tighten, including as measured by the ECB-BIG
Despite the very sharp widening of sovereign spreads between 28 September and 2 October, financial conditions as measured by the
MF-FCI did not tighten, with sovereign yields actually falling over this period (with the exception of the OAT and the BTP), for example.

Nevertheless, the high volatility of the bond market, particularly in the sovereign segment, is likely to continue to affect financial
conditions. Looking slightly further ahead, the ECB’s key interest rate rises — the one that occurred on 16 September and another that we
anticipate for December 2026 (both +25 bp) — are likely to result in a more pronounced tightening. With a time lag of a number of weeks,
financial conditions, as defined by the ECB-BIG, are also expected to tighten. The price effect is likely to lead to a negative volume effect

on this occasion.

Document 329: France_ double-digit growth in business creations, a slowdown in the rise in bankruptcies and stable non-performing loans ratio (2026-10-08)

Source PDF: all report pdf/France_ double-digit growth in business creations, a slowdown in the rise in bankruptcies and stable non-performing loans ratio (2026-10-08).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-08
Pages: 1
Status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 8 October 2026

EcoFlash

France: double-digit growth in business creations, a slowdown in the rise in bankruptcies and 
stable non-performing loans ratio

Business creations rose by 12% y/y over the first eight months of 2026 1. This is significantly higher than the 5.3% y/y 
growth recorded in 2025. This increase was driven by sectors linked to information and communication technologies.

Meanwhile, business bankruptcies rose by 5.3% y/y between January and July, according to data from the Banque de 
France (compared with the 3.6% y/y increase for the whole of 2025). The rise was more pronounced in the 1st quarter (+8% y/y) 
before slowing down. This slowdown could persist, as, according to Altares, payment delays have fallen to an average of 12.3 
days in the second quarter of 2026, compared with 14.1 days a year earlier (and 13.1 days for the European average).

Rising bankruptcies are more heavily affecting the services sector, which is also experiencing slightly stronger growth 
in business creations. This correlation is particularly noticeable in the retail sector, as business creations have risen by 13% 
since the start of 2026, while bankruptcies are up by 3%. In construction and manufacturing, business bankruptcies have been 
falling since the second quarter of 2026.

The average ratio of non -performing corporate loans has remained stable at a moderately high level for the past year  
(4.0% of total loans and advances in Q2 2026). In construction, where activity is more cyclical than in other sectors, the ra tio of 
non-performing loans has reached its highest level since 2019, standing at 9.5%.

The proportion of loans that have deteriorated but are not yet non-performing (“Stage 2” according to IFRS 9 terminology) 
is stable, standing at 15.4% q/q, and remains below its all -time high (16.1%). This indicator, which helps to anticipate trends in 
non-performing loans, is relatively encouraging.

Despite a significant increase since 2021, the economic weight of bankruptcies remains, on average, below its peak in 
Q1 2025 (0.53 compared with 0.61, respectively). This indicator generally allows bankruptcies to be weighted based on the size 
of the businesses. However, the economic weight of bankrupted small businesses is at a historically high level (2.11).

Take a look at our EcoCharts on trends in business creations and bankruptcies in France and the 
associated risks. It will be updated on a quarterly basis.

Thomas Humblot 
Economiste, team: Banking economics 
thomas.humblot@bnpparibas.com

Stéphane Colliac 
Head of Advanced economies, France 
stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

1 For more details, see Business creations and bankruptcies in France: double-digit growth in business creations, lower growth in bankruptcies, 
and stable non-performing loans, 8 October 2026.

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The six-month annualised growth rate in the Westpac–
Melbourne Institute Leading Index, which indicates the
likely pace of economic activity relative to trend three to
nine months into the future, held at –0.03% in September,
unchanged from August.

The Leading Index continues to show moderately improved
momentum compared to the middle of the year, with the
latest reads pointing to growth holding at or slightly below
trend heading into late 2026/early 2027. Growth appears
to be stabilising at a slow pace rather than stalling. That
said, both the latest fuel prices and the RBA’s September
interest rate increase are still working their way through to
the economy with impacts likely to take some of the gloss off

growth near term. Overall, Westpac expects annual growth
to come in at 1.5% for calendar 2026, a below-trend result.
The Leading Index growth rate has firmed slightly over the
last six months, rising from –0.16% back in March to –0.03%
in September. The component detail shows mixed moves over
the period. Four components have contributed more positively
with reduced drags from the Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Unemployment Expectations Index (adding +0.23ppts to

the Index growth rate); the Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Consumer Expectations Index (adding another +0.15ppts);
and the S&P/ASX200 (+0.15ppts) combining with a modest
lift in growth in US industrial production (adding +0.12ppts).
These positives have been partially offset by modest drags
from four previously stable components: dwelling approvals
(–0.34ppts); monthly hours worked (–0.08ppts); commodity
prices, measured in AUD terms (–0.07ppts) and the yield

spread (–0.05ppts).
Overall, the component mix speaks to a lack of direction.
Indeed, despite some large shocks over the last year, the
Leading Index growth rate has been relatively contained since
early 2025, holding in a narrow –0.3% to +0.5% range. This
compares to history which, outside of the COVID and GFC
shocks, has seen growth rates move between a much wider
–1.9% to +1.6% range. Of course, the apparent stability,
which is evident across all components of the index, may not

last but for now it suggests the economy has been stuck in a
bit of a holding pattern over the last two years.
Leading Index still tracking a touch below trend
The Westpac-Melbourne Institute Leading Index of
Economic Activity is designed to assist in identifying
turning points in the economy. The index combines
variables that reflect different aspects of the economy
into a single index that generally produces a more reliable
cyclical indicator than any single component taken

individually. The Leading Index of Economic Activity
provides advance information on the state of the economy
and gives early warnings of cyclical turning points.
Matthew Hassan
Head of Australian Macro-Forecasting
**Please note that this will be the last Westpac press release for
the monthly Westpac-Melbourne Institute Leading Index. We will
continue to publish regular bulletin updates through our westpaciq.
com.au website.

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

Bank of Thailand (BOT) (October 28)

We expect the BOT to maintain its policy 
rate at 1.00% at its 28 October meeting, 
extending its pause for a fourth 
consecutive decision as it balances 
inflationary pressures against uneven 
and subdued economic growth. Our view 
is aligned with fixed market income 
pricing. Headline inflation rose anew to 
2.8% yoy in September 2026, driven by 
higher energy, food, and core price 
increases, but remained within the upper 
end of the central bank’s 1 -3% target 
range. As the BOT is still likely to look 
through what it views as temporary, 
supply-driven inflationary pressu res, it 
will likely refrain from tightening policy, 
and keep interest rates accommodative 
to support the soft and uneven growth 
cycle.

European Central Bank (October 29)

We expect the ECB to extend its pause, 
keeping the deposit facility rate at 2.5%. 
Market participants will instead focus on 
the central bank’s assessment of rising 
French and Italian bond yields and 
widening spreads versus Bunds. While 
we do not expect dir ect references to 
individual countries, the ECB is likely to 
signal that recent developments in 
France reflect the country’s weaker 
fiscal and debt profile rather than a 
broader systemic concern. Policymakers 
will seek to differentiate between a 
localised bond sell -off and a Eurozone -
wide market disruption, justifying their 
decision to monitor developments rather 
than implement specific measures at this 
juncture. Meanwhile, inflation continues 
to rise, with little relief expected in 
upcoming readings given elevated 
global fuel prices. 
Bank of Japan (October 30)

The Bank of Japan is expected to keep 
the overnight call rate unchanged at 1.25% 
at this meeting, pausing after the 25bp 
hike in September. The BOJ is likely to 
assess the impact of the previous rate 
hike before taking further action. The 
recent stabilisat ion of the yen also 
reduces the urgency of another hike at 
this meeting.

The tightening bias should remain intact. 
Growth and inflation remain broadly on 
track, leaving room for the BOJ to revise 
up its FY2026 –FY2027 GDP and CPI 
forecasts. Wage growth has also 
remained strong, with Rengo reportedly 
targeting wage increases of 6% in 2027, 
reinforcing the BOJ’s confidence that 
underlying inflation can be sustained 
around its 2% target. We continue to 
expect the policy rate to rise to 1.50% in 
December 2026 or January 2027, 
followed by a further increase to 1.75% 
by 2Q 2027.

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Hong Kong SAR

Export growth is expected to accelerate 
from 53.0% yoy in August to 55.0% in 
September, in line with the pickup in 
China’s export growth from 25.0% to 
36.3%. As a key re-export hub for China,

### Page 3

The Week Ahead October 23, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

Hong Kong should benefit from robust 
AI-related electronics trade, with 
electrical machinery, equipment and 
parts exports to Hong Kong accounting 
for 20.6% of China’s total exports. 
Import growth is also expected to remain 
firm, supported by resilient expo rt 
orders and gradually improving 
investment sentiment, sustaining 
demand for intermediate and capital 
goods.

Singapore

Singapore’s industrial production (IP) 
likely remained in expansion for a 13th 
consecutive month in September 2026, 
although we expect growth to moderate 
to 7.6% yoy from August’s strong 15.4% 
yoy pace. IP growth continued to be 
underpinned by the electronics and 
precision engineering clusters, which 
has been key drivers of Singapore’s 
manufacturing strength, reflecting 
sustained demand for artificial 
intelligence-related hardware. However, 
performance across clusters remained 
uneven, with chemicals and biomedical 
manufacturing likely contracting, and 
tempering overall gains.

South Korea

The preliminary estimate for 3Q26 GDP 
is likely to show growth remaining solid 
at 3.2% yoy , slightly below 3.7% in 2Q 
and 3.8% in 1Q. Nominal GDP growth is 
likely to remain in double digits, 
significantly outpacing real GDP growth, 
thanks to improved terms of trade amid 
rising memory export prices. By 
expenditure, exports picked up in 3Q in 
both nominal and real terms, supported 
by stronger semiconductor shipments. 
Investment also appeared robust, as 
reflected in the equipment investment 
estimation index and machinery order 
index. Private consumption, however, 
appeared to weaken amid the sharp 
KOSPI correction since July following  
the introduction of leveraged ETF 
regulations. Our full -year GDP growth 
forecasts of 3.5% for 2026 and 2.4% for 
2027 remain on track.

Taiwan

The preliminary estimate for 3Q GDP is 
likely to show growth remaining strong 
at 12 –13% yoy , close to 12.9% in 2Q, 
albeit below the peak of 15.4% in 1Q. 
Exports maintained strong double -digit 
growth in 3Q (44.6% yoy  in nominal 
terms and 21.1% in real terms), supported 
by robust shipments of semiconductors, 
servers and other AI -related products. 
Machinery investment also sustained 
strong double-digit growth, underpinned 
by higher capacity utilisation in the ICT 
sector and a resulting increase in capital 
goods imports. Private consumption 
appeared to have strengthened further, 
supported by stock market gains, 
improving wage growth, and the 
associated wealth and income effects. 
Construction investment remained the 
weak s pot, weighed down by elevated 
inventories in the new-home market and 
the ongoing need for inventory digestion. 
We maintain our 2026 and 2027 GDP 
growth forecasts at 11.6% and 5.6%, 
respectively, with risks tilted to the 
upside.

### Page 2

An update on AI and US equities

Both stocks and investor views relating to the AI trade have been exceptionally 
volatile during the last few months. Most of the volatility has been concentrated in the 
AI infrastructure complex, but the latest earnings season also drove sharp moves in the 
hyperscalers and among software stocks. 
Below we address three of the most common AI-related questions we have received 
from clients in recent weeks: 
1. What is the outlook for AI capex? 
2. What revenues are required to generate a sufficient return on recent AI 
investments? 
3. What outlook for AI is the equity market pricing?

1. What is the outlook for AI capex? 
The largest US hyperscalers are on track to spend $800 billion in capex in 2026 and 
consensus estimates imply they will spend $1.1 trillion in 2027. Consensus 2027 
capex estimates have been revised higher by $175 billion since the start of the Q2 
earnings season. Commentary from the hyperscalers during earnings season described a 
supply-constrained environment and large revenue backlogs, and these factors are 
driving the companies to tap both debt and equity markets to support additional 
investment spending. Based on consensus estimates, capex in 2027 is on track to reach a

### Page 3

larger share of GDP than any technological investment cycle since the railroad build-out 
in the late 1800’s. 
Our baseline forecast is that hyperscaler capex spending will increase substantially 
in 2027 but that the rate of both capex growth and upside surprises to consensus 
estimates will diminish relative to recent quarters. GS equity analysts estimate that 
hyperscaler capex will register $1.2 trillion in 2027 and $1.4 trillion in 2028. Their 
estimates are $130 billion and $100 billion above the consensus estimates, respectively, 
but nonetheless imply a deceleration in capex growth from nearly 100% in 2026 to 54% 
in 2027 and 12% in 2028.

The need for external financing and potential supply bottlenecks will likely 
constrain the rate of capex growth next year. Hyperscaler capex spending now 
exceeds cash flow from operations, which means large further growth will increasingly 
need funding via debt and equity issuance. While hyperscalers have added leverage, 
their balance sheets do not appear to be a binding constraint and their interest coverage 
ratios remain extremely healthy. Our credit strategists instead point to concentration in 
the investment grade market as the more relevant bottleneck, which will require 
additional funding from private credit and equity issuance. Potential government 
restrictions on data center construction or an intentional slowdown in frontier model 
roll-out could also act as potential constraints on spending, as could shortages of labor, 
power, memory, or other inputs. Constraints on capex represent a risk to the near-term 
earnings of AI infrastructure beneficiaries, but could help ease investor concerns about 
overbuilding.

### Page 4

Going forward, token demand growth will need to outpace declining token prices to 
support continued growth in investment spending. Frontier models are currently a 
key source of demand for hyperscaler compute. However, the rise of competitive 
open-source models has contributed to a decline in average token prices. Measures of 
frontier token demand slowed in July, simultaneous with sell-offs of many AI 
infrastructure stocks, but accelerated again in August. 
For most AI infrastructure companies, decelerating AI investment spending 
suggests decelerating EPS growth. AI infrastructure companies, and semis in 
particular, have been the clearest beneficiaries of the capex surge. Continued upward 
revisions to consensus 2027 capex estimates should support continued positive 
revisions to consensus 2027 EPS estimates for many AI infrastructure stocks, but the 
magnitude of revisions will likely diminish relative to what occurred in H1 2026.

### Page 5

2. What revenues are required to generate a return on AI investments? 
Our equity analysts estimate that the hyperscalers would need to generate annual 
AI revenues of roughly $300 billion in the next few years to break even on their 
average annual capex investments in 2026 and 2027. These estimates reflect their 
expectations for average annual AI capex in 2026 and 2027 for the US hyperscalers, 
including AMZN, GOOGL, META, MSFT, ORCL, and SPCX. The breakeven revenues 
required for this capex represent the sum of annual depreciation and operating 
expenses. Exhibit 8 provides an estimated sensitivity of required AI revenues the 
hyperscalers must generate in different return on invested capital (“ROIC”) scenarios. 
These required revenue estimates are anchored on average annual capex in 2026 and 
2027 and would argue for even larger estimates based on cumulative capex.

lines represent equal-weighted averages of growth implied by Anera and OpenRouter data

### Page 6

The hyperscalers’ current estimated AI revenues remain below the amount that 
would allow them to break even on their capex, but those revenues are growing 
quickly and revenue backlogs are sizable. The hyperscalers have started to monetize 
their AI investments. Year/year cloud revenue growth for AMZN, GOOGL, MSFT, and 
ORCL has accelerated from 25% in 2024 to 48% in Q2 2026. In dollar terms, annualized 
cloud revenues in excess of their 2024 growth rate registered nearly $70 billion in Q2 
and have been roughly doubling during the past few quarters. The three major US public 
cloud companies (AMZN, GOOGL, MSFT) have also announced a combined revenue 
backlog of $1.7 trillion, which provides line of sight into these companies’ ability to meet 
required revenue targets.

### Page 7

The magnitude of capex in 2026 and 2027 points to even larger “required” annual 
revenues in the next few years for the “application layer” of companies buying 
much of that compute from the hyperscalers. These revenues reflect the additional 
costs and profit margins of application companies on top of the “required” hyperscaler 
revenues selling compute. EBIT margins for large-cap software companies have 
averaged 40% during the past five years. We utilize EBIT margins rather than gross 
margins to include the costs of both training and inference, and assume additional 
expenses alongside compute. The range of potential required revenues is wide 
depending on which companies constitute the application layer and how their costs are 
structured.  
Exhibit 11 provides a range of potential “required” revenues based on hyperscaler 
ROIC and application layer EBIT margins. For example, the application layer would 
have to generate over $1 trillion in annual revenues if those companies had a 30% EBIT 
margin and the hyperscalers generated a 10%-20% return on invested capital. 
The “required” application layer revenues that would justify current capex are large 
but potentially achievable. For example, estimates of global advertising and software 
spending each equal roughly $1.5 trillion in 2026. Our economists estimate that total 
annual AI US labor productivity gains that will potentially accrue to capital total roughly 
$1.8 trillion. However, not all of these gains will necessarily accrue to the infrastructure 
and application layer companies. In addition, another year of strong capex growth in 
2028 would raise the hurdle further. AI-related revenues and capex needs would be 
substantially larger accounting for physical AI or the potential emergence of AGI.

### Page 8

3. What outlook for AI is the equity market pricing? 
While market participants express skepticism about the durability of AI 
infrastructure stocks’ earnings growth, that view is at least partially priced. The 
median AI infrastructure stock has de-rated from 32x consensus forward earnings in 
April 2026 to 22x today. In addition to tracking standard P/E multiples, comparing 
recent changes in market cap to recent changes in earnings can signal how long the 
market expects current earnings strength to persist. Among various AI infrastructure 
baskets, that ratio remains most elevated among optical networking, electrical grid, and 
liquid cooling stocks while expectations appear most conservative among 
semiconductors and utilities. 
Semiconductor valuations appear to embed particularly skeptical views regarding 
the sustainability of current earnings strength. Valuations have declined sharply 
among Semis stocks, which is typical among cyclical companies with strong near-term 
growth but uncertain medium-term growth. We believe the investment opportunity is 
more compelling in fabless chip stocks than in memory stocks. Our equity analysts are 
Buy-rated on NVDA and AVGO but Neutral-rated on MU.

### Page 9

Memory stock prices imply earnings will decline in coming years, but the degree of 
pessimism does not appear particularly extreme relative to past cycles. Memory 
stocks currently trade at a 2-year forward P/E of 4x, half their 15-year average, but 
Memory firms currently enjoy gross margins of roughly 80%, more than double their 
historical average. To bring the current P/E of memory stocks to its 15-year average, 
earnings in coming years would need to decline by roughly 50%. While this decline is 
large in absolute terms, memory is a cyclical industry and has experienced larger EPS 
declines, including outright negative earnings, during previous cycles. This pricing will 
prove too pessimistic if 2028 forecasts are realized, but many investors have expressed 
skepticism about rotating back into higher beta pockets of the infrastructure trade as 
capex growth begins to normalize.

### Page 10

In contrast, the fabless chip stocks also trade at discounted valuations but have had 
less extreme margin expansion. Margins for the largest fabless chip stocks are only 
modestly above their historical average. Earnings in the next few years would need to 
decline by about 10% to bring the P/E back to its historical average. This is similar to the 
magnitude of historical realized cyclical earnings declines for the group.

The likelihood of decelerating capex growth should be a positive catalyst for the 
hyperscalers. We previously laid out three conditions for the hyperscalers to regain 
leadership. These include decelerating macroeconomic growth, demonstrated 
monetization of AI investment, and decelerating capex growth. Our economists believe 
US economic growth is no longer accelerating. The hyperscalers have already started to 
demonstrate signs of monetization, and we expect capex growth will slow in 2027. 
Uncertainty about the length, magnitude, and return on investment for this capex cycle 
will likely persist. However, the hyperscalers have de-rated to their lowest P/E multiples 
in over a decade, and the group now trades just four turns above the median S&P 500 
company, its smallest premium on record. While investors have recently been focused 
more on the hyperscalers’ negative free cash flows than on their earnings, visibility into 
improving FCF as revenues accelerate and capex decelerates should be supportive for 
the stocks.

### Page 11

We expect the progression of the AI trade towards application roll-out will remain a 
source of return dispersion. We believe the “winners” and “losers” from the application 
layer will take several quarters to play out, as AI applications get built and adoption 
broadens. 
The threat of disruption had weighed on the share prices of many Software stocks 
earlier this year, but the group has rebounded sharply in recent weeks. Reported 
Software fundamentals have shown little sign of AI disruption, with annual recurring 
revenue growth for the median stock in GS analyst coverage accelerating slightly in 
recent quarters. A group of Software stocks has rallied by 45% and is now within 8% of 
its high reached in late 2025. Software stocks focused on IT infrastructure and 
cybersecurity have posted the strongest returns, while enterprise management software 
remain below their previous highs.

### Page 12

Consumer-facing businesses with high switching costs have also recently emerged 
as a potential area of disruption. The increasing use of agents, including by new 
consumer-facing tools such as Muse, may disrupt the business models of some 
consumer firms. Our colleagues in Global Banking & Markets developed a basket of 
“consumer inertia” stocks (GSXUSWCH) where user stickiness is driven more by effort 
required to take action rather than product differentiation. The basket includes 
insurance, telecom, travel, subscriptions, marketplaces, and fintech. The basket has 
lagged a sector-neutral hedge by 5 pp since mid-September.

### Page 13

Enterprise AI adoption remains in early stages but spending has recently 
accelerated, suggesting the impact of AI on corporate earnings should grow 
increasingly visible in coming quarters. The Census Bureau survey suggests that 40% 
of large US firms are using AI in the production of goods and services. Our analysis of Q2 
earnings commentary suggests roughly 50% of S&P 500 companies are using AI in the 
context of productivity, but very few companies have quantified the productivity uplift 
today. However, the revenue run rates of model providers and alternative measures of 
company spending on AI have accelerated during the last several months alongside the 
increasing use of agentic harnesses. 
In contrast with past periods of market exuberance, the equity market today does 
not appear to be pricing expectations of widespread productivity gains. Baskets of 
AI productivity beneficiaries have traded roughly sideways on a relative basis YTD, 
matching the trajectory of near-term forward EPS estimates. Similarly, the measure of 
market-implied long-term growth expectations in Exhibit 22 is only slightly above the 
historical average (for details, see US Equity Views: The next phases of the AI trade).

《地緣風險變數使油價短期仍偏上》
OPEC+決議11月產出配額維持 9月規模，預期目前
配額將持續至今年年底 ，下一輪恢復最後一套 200
萬桶/日的額外減產措施將在明年視市況調整。依市
場推估，中東區域海運原油及成品油運輸出口量9月
底曾回至接近九成 ，受荷姆茲海峽油輪襲擊事件增
加，伊朗革命衛隊預告管理範圍擴大 ，使原油運輸
開始下降 ，但曼德海峽因沙國駐葉門大使稱已清除
胡塞武裝勢力 ，預期曼德海峽運輸量將不受影響 。
但受轉運延長運輸時間 、避開受制裁的原油採購 ，
加上搶補低庫存 ，市場追逐現貨使布油呈現大幅現

貨溢價，七國集團(G7)雖計畫在未來四個月內釋放
總計原油與柴油戰略儲備各 0.5億桶，總計1億桶，
但預計佔每天原油用量低 ，僅短期壓抑油價 ，主要
重點仍在美伊談判、美國向中東增兵、伊朗襲船，
市場投資展望
資產 4Q26看法 投資展望

原物料 中立 受供應下降可能性增，較看好銅價，但留意銅關稅政策。

荷姆茲海峽運輸仍充滿變數 ，布油價格將維持在 90
美元之上較長時間 ，但若衝突緩解 ，油價雖可回落 ，

《天然氣價格：美持平或略低於去年 ，歐洲偏上行 》
美國鑽井活動緩升 ，增加較多在天然氣 ，隨美國新
的液化天然氣出口工廠上線 ，逐漸改善管道瓶頸 ，
天然氣 10月庫存僅高於五年均值 2%，注入期可持
續增至11月中，預估今年冬天暖氣度日數將持平去
年 ， 並 比 過 去 五 個 冬 季 的 均 值 低1% ， 預估美國
4Q26天然氣價格將因季節性走升，但持平或略低於
去年。卡達能源LNG船已出口 ，但非往歐洲 ，歐洲
儲氣量雖拉升 ，但僅達 70%，遠低於過往 ，TTF價
格須優於亞洲以吸引運往亞洲的 LNG轉往歐洲 ，加

上中東地緣緊張，預期4Q26 TTF價格仍有上行風險。
重點摘要
• 天然氣：美國天然氣出口增
加，庫存增速下降 ，使4Q26
美國天然氣價格持平或略低
於去年同期；歐洲因庫存偏
低的回補需求將使天然氣價
格偏上行。

2
《釋儲及放寬制裁增加柴油恐使煉油利潤見頂》
受歐美老舊煉油廠關廠 ，煉油產能因俄烏 、中東戰
事受損，估計全球煉油廠開工率年減近 800萬桶/日，
加上中、俄禁止柴油出口 ，使柴油供應比原油更緊
張。美國柴油庫存低於五年區間下緣 ，柴油價格創
高，因美未禁止柴油出口 ，柴油利潤保持強勁 。但
美協調G7國家釋出柴油儲備為期四個月 ，達成美俄

柴油供應協議增加供給 ，雖粗略換算為短期每日供
給增加不到全球 1%，預期對柴油價格壓力影響僅
5%內，明年隨設備漸修復、地緣風險降低後，才緩
降，但美國超額柴油利潤恐見頂。
《4Q26能源類股較看好綜合油企》
預估美綜合油企 4Q26受惠油價高於預期 、LNG出
口增加，柴油高裂解價差 ，但進入汽油需求淡季 ，
獲利季比下滑 ，但年比仍大幅成長 ，現金流仍強大 ，
維持資本紀律及股東回報 。雖成品油供應仍較原油
吃緊，但煉油商超額利潤恐見頂 ，較看好綜合油企 。

《預期金價短期不利因素多，但中期緩升》
近月金價歷經 美國通膨及經濟成長 ，美國FED採取
預防管理式升息一碼 ，認為年底前仍有升息一碼空
間，但受就業降溫及通膨低於預期 ，市場降低10月
升息預期 ，加上法國國債被拋售蔓延全球債市使美
長期公債殖利率及美元走強 、黃金淨多頭部位下降 ，
壓低本月金價下跌 ，也跌破季線 。預期短線金價受
債券殖利率飆升 、實質利率走升 及美元走強 相對不
利金價走勢 ，震盪放大。但若美債收益率回落 、擔
憂債務可持續性 、分散交易對手風險及多元化資產
配置，中國人行連續23個月購金，並加大購金步伐 ，
3Q26黃金ETF呈現創紀錄淨買超 ，預期3800-4000
美元有撐，維持金價中期緩步上行。

今月の景気判断 ～持ち直している
【今月のポイント】
 東海経済は、持ち直している
 7月の生産（東海3県、季節調整値）は前月
比＋2.2％と2ヵ月ぶりに増加した。生産水
準も高く、増加基調にあるとみられる
 輸出は総じてみると足踏み状態。先行きは
持ち直しを見込むが、米・イランの対立長
期化などで足踏みとなるリスクがある
 日銀短観9月調査の業況判断DI（「良い」
－「悪い」）は、製造業が改善する一方、非
製造業は悪化した。設備投資も前年度比
増加が見込まれている
 8月の小売販売額は名目で2ヵ月連続で
前年比プラスとなったが、実質では3ヵ月
連続でマイナス。一方、新車販売台数は
5ヵ月連続で前年比プラスとなった
現状

〒105-8501
東京都港区虎ノ門5-11-2 オランダヒルズ森タワー

Document 514: トルコ中銀は６会合ぶりの利下げも、金融市場の混乱が新たな懸念材料に ～インフレ鈍化で引き締め度合いを調整も、株安によるリ_1298312e

Source PDF: all report pdf/トルコ中銀は６会合ぶりの利下げも、金融市場の混乱が新たな懸念材料に ～インフレ鈍化で引き締め度合いを調整も、株安によるリ_1298312e.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-23
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年10 月 23 日

1/3 
トルコ中銀は６会合ぶりの利下げも、金融市場の混乱が新たな懸念材料に 
～インフレ鈍化で引き締め度合いを調整も、株安によるリラ相場や実体経済への波及に警戒～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ トルコ中銀は 10 月 22 日の金融政策委員会において、政策金利(１週間物レポ金利)を 100bp
引き下げ 36.00％とした。利下げは 6 会合ぶりで、金利水準は 2023 年 10 月以来の低さとな
る。背景には、原油高や通貨安への懸念があったにもかかわらず、９月のインフレ率が前年
同月比＋29.73％と 2021 年 11 月以来の低い伸びになるなど、物価の鈍化が着実に進んでい
ることがある。実質金利のプラス幅が拡大して引き締め度合いが強まったため、今回の利下
げは、引き締め姿勢を維持しつつその度合いを調整する従来の動きに沿ったものといえる。 
○ 声明文は過去５会合をほぼ踏襲した。基調的な物価の減速を認める一方、地政学リスクによ
る商品市況の上昇をインフレ見通しの上振れリスクとして警戒し、インフレが著しく悪化し
た場合の再利上げの可能性にも言及しており、「タカ派」姿勢は維持されている。 
○ 金融市場では、 投資信託の流動性を巡る問題に加え、MSCI が 10 月15 日にトルコ株の一部指
数変更を暫定凍結し、2027 年6 月までに市場改革の進展が確認できなければ新興国市場から
フロンティア市場への分類変更を協議する方針を示したことから、主要株価指数は下落基調
にある。市場の混乱が長期化すれば、家計の外貨・金への資産移動を通じたリラ安圧力や、
実体経済・政策運営への悪影響が懸念されるため、今後の動向に注意が必要である。

トルコ中央銀行は、10 月 22 日に開催した定例の金融政策委員会で、政策金利である１週間物レ
ポ金利を 100bp 引き下げて 36.00％とすることを決定した。同行による利下げ実施は６会合ぶりで
あり、政策金利も2023 年 10 月以来の低水準となる。背景には、同国のインフレ率が着実に鈍化し
ていることがある。

### Page 2

2/3 
イラン情勢の悪化を受けた原油高が世
界的なエネルギーインフレを招くなか、
原油需要の大部分を輸入に依存する同
国では、その影響が懸念されてきた。さ
らに、原油高は輸入額の増加による対外
収支の悪化を招いており、リラの対ドル
相場はジリ安の展開が続く。そのため、
輸入物価の押し上げもインフレ圧力を
増幅させることが懸念された。しかし、
直近９月のインフレ率は前年同月比＋
29.73％と 2021 年 11 月以来の低い伸び
となった（図１） 。 
同行は 2024 年 12 月に現在の経済チー
ムの下で初の利下げに踏み切り、その後
も実質金利をプラスに維持する抑制的
なスタンスを維持しながら、断続的な利
下げに動いてきた。 このところはインフ
レ率の鈍化を受けて、実質金利のプラス
幅が拡大するなど引き締め度合いが強
まる動きが確認されていた （図２） 。 この
ため、今回の利下げ決定は、引き締め姿
勢を維持しつつ、その度合いを調整して
きた動きに沿ったものと捉えられる。 
会合後に公表した声明文では、 物価動向について 「足元の動きや先行指標は基調的な減速が続い
ていることを裏付けている」との見方を示した。そのうえで「経済指標や供給ショックによる物価
への影響が限定的なものにとどまることは内需の弱さを反映している」 とした。 一方で、 「地政学リ
スクによる商品市況の上昇はインフレ見通しの上振れリスクになる 」として、 「地政学リスクが物
価動向、経済活動、インフレ期待に与える影響を注視する」と、前回会合と同じ見方を示した。 
先行きの政策運営について 「需要動向やリラ相場、 インフレ期待を通じた物価安定を実現するま
で引き締めスタンスを維持する」との従来からの姿勢を維持した。具体的な政策についても「イン
フレ見通しと実績、 基調的な動きを勘案して決定する」 、 「会合ごとにインフレ見通しを注視しつつ
慎重に見直す」 とし、 「インフレ見通しが著しく、 かつ持続的に悪化した場合は引き締め姿勢を強め
る」 、 「インフレの上振れリスクに引き続き高い注意を払っている」と、引き続き再利上げに動く可
能性をあらためて強調した。 
また、 「信用市場や預金市場で予期せぬ事態が発生した場合は、 流動性を巡る状況を注視しつつ、
マクロプルーデンス政策を強化する」 と従来からの考えを繰り返した。 そのうえで、 「インフレ目標

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
の実現に向けて、 入手可能なデータに基づく透明性の高い枠組みの下で必要な環境整備を図るべく
政策決定を行う」との考えをあらためて示している。声明文の内容は、過去５会合をほぼ踏襲して
いるうえ、 過去４会合と同様にインフレの上振れリスクに対する警戒感を強調しており、 「タカ派」
姿勢を維持している。 
一方で、 トルコ金融市場では、主要株価指数であるイスタンブール100 指数 （BIST100） が下落基
調をたどるなど、不安定な動きが続いている（注１）（図３） 。背景には、一部の投資信託の流動性
問題をきっかけとする売り圧力に加え、
指数算出会社の MSCI が 10 月 15 日、ト
ルコ株式に関する一部の指数変更を暫
定的に凍結し、2027 年６月までに十分な
市場改革の進展が確認できなければ、新
興国市場からフロンティア市場などへ
の分類変更に向けた協議を検討する方
針を示したことがある（注２） 。 こうした
市場の混乱は家計部門のリスク回避姿
勢を強め、外貨や金などへの資産移動を
通じてリラ相場 の下押し圧力につなが
る可能性もある。市場の混乱が長期化すれば、実体経済への悪影響が顕在化するとともに、政府の
政策運営にも影響が及ぶおそれがあり、先行きはその動向に注意を払う必要性が高まっている。

Document 562: 兆易创新Q3净利润同比增长729%，环比下降22%，朱一明、葛卫东没卖 _ 财报见闻

Source PDF: all report pdf/兆易创新Q3净利润同比增长729%，环比下降22%，朱一明、葛卫东没卖 _ 财报见闻.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

前三季度，公司实现营业收⼊ 202.97 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 197.11% ；归⺟净利润 110.66 亿元，
同⽐增⻓ 921.55% 。其中，第三季度营收同⽐增⻓ 225.65% ，归⺟净利润同⽐增⻓
728.93% ，但净利润从第⼆季度的 53.96 亿元降⾄ 42.09 亿元，环⽐下降约 22% 。公司创始
⼈朱⼀明、知名 “ ⽜散 ” 葛卫东第三季度持股数量均未发⽣变化。
摘要：
存储芯⽚涨价潮推动兆易创新前三季度业绩⼤幅增⻓，但单季度盈利已出现环⽐回落。
10 ⽉ 29 ⽇晚，兆易创新披露 2026 年第三季度报告。前三季度，公司实现营业收⼊ 202.97 亿元，
同⽐增⻓ 197.11% ；归⺟净利润 110.66 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 921.55% ；扣⾮归⺟净利润 94.34 亿元，
同⽐增⻓ 805.26% 。业绩⼤幅增⻓主要受益于存储芯⽚量价⻬升，⽑利率和经营现⾦流同步改
善。
不 过 ， 单 季 度 业 绩 出 现 环 ⽐ 回 落 。 第 三 季 度 ， 公 司 实 现 营 业 收 ⼊ 87.32 亿 元 ， 同 ⽐ 增 ⻓

225.65% ；归⺟净利润 42.09 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 728.93% ，但较第⼆季度的 53.96 亿元下降约
22% 。扣⾮归⺟净利润为 45.51 亿元，同⽐增⻓ 814.39% ，⾼于当季归⺟净利润。
股东持仓⽅⾯，公司创始⼈朱⼀明、知名 “ ⽜散 ” 葛卫东第三季度持股数量均未发⽣变化；葛卫东
的⼀致⾏动⼈王萍则⼩幅减持 6000 股。
存储芯⽚量价⻬升，前三季度⽑利率升⾄ 66%
兆易创新表⽰，报告期内存储芯⽚⾏业供给紧张，公司存储芯⽚产品量价⻬升，存储业务持续
向好；与此同时， MCU 等多个业务产品线持续发⼒，产品出货规模实现较好增⻓。
产品价格和出货增⻓带动公司盈利能⼒显著提升。根据财报数据计算，兆易创新前三季度综合
⽑利率约为 66.22% ，较上年同期提⾼约 27.64 个百分点。
现⾦流也明显改善。前三季度，公司经营活动产⽣的现⾦流量净额为 101.24 亿元，同⽐增⻓
463.81% 。截⾄ 9 ⽉末，公司存货账⾯价值为 62.92 亿元，较上年末增⻓约 105.22% 。

⾦融资产公允价值变动影响利润， Q3 净利环⽐下降约 22%

图⽚
除主营业务增⻓外，⾦融资产相关损益也对公司利润产⽣明显影响。前三季度，兆易创新公允
价值变动及相关⾦融资产处置损益约为 18.79 亿元，⾮经常性损益合计 16.32 亿元。
第三季度，公司⾮经常性损益合计为负 3.42 亿元，其中⾦融资产公允价值变动及处置损益约为负
3.53 亿元。这也是当季扣⾮归⺟净利润 45.51 亿元⾼于归⺟净利润 42.09 亿元的重要原因。
从单季度表现看，第三季度归⺟净利润环⽐下降约 22% ，⽽扣⾮归⺟净利润仍保持同⽐⾼速增
⻓。主营业务增⻓与⾦融资产相关损益的变化，共同影响了当季利润表现。
前⼗⼤股东持仓调整，朱⼀明、葛卫东持股未变
截⾄ 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇，兆易创新前⼗⼤股东中，部分股东调整了持仓，另有两名股东新进⼊前⼗⼤股东

名单。
其中，华夏国证半导体芯⽚ ETF 增持 59.85 万股，持股数量增⾄ 564.10 万股；⾹港中央结算有限
公司减持 499.30 万股，持股数量降⾄ 3316.87 万股。永赢先锋半导体智选混合型发起式证券投资
基⾦、深圳市百业源投资有限公司新进前⼗⼤股东，分别持有 350 万股和 343.46 万股。
公司创始⼈朱⼀明的持股数量在第三季度保持不变。截⾄ 9 ⽉末，朱⼀明持有兆易创新 3464.74
万股，占总股本的 4.92% 。
今年 5 ⽉⾄ 6 ⽉，朱⼀明曾累计减持 1111.06 万股，减持⾦额约 44 亿元。此后，他承诺⾃ 7 ⽉ 29 ⽇
起 12 个⽉内不减持公司股份，并计划于今年 12 ⽉ 13 ⽇⾄明年 7 ⽉ 29 ⽇期间增持不低于 10 亿元。
知 名 “ ⽜ 散 ” 葛 卫 东 第 三 季 度 同 样 未 调 整 持 仓 ， 仍 持 有 兆 易 创 新 1644.67 万 股 ， 占 总 股 本 的
2.34% 。其⼀致⾏动⼈王萍持股较第⼆季度末减少 6000 股，两⼈截⾄ 9 ⽉末合计持有约 2504.66

万股，占总股本的约 3.56% 。
此外，兆易创新表⽰，将于 11 ⽉ 17 ⽇举⾏ 2026 年第三季度业绩说明会。

事件  1
10/29 歐洲央⾏維持存款利率 2.50% ，拉加德宣布明年 2 ⽉提前卸任
五⼤重點
1   歐洲央⾏（ ECB ） 10/29 ⼀致決議維持存款機制利率 2.50% ，為 9 ⽉升息 1 碼後⾸度按兵不動；
總裁拉加德表⽰管委會曾討論本次是否升息，最終認為⽬前⾜以等待更多資料。
2   聲明指出能源價格持續上漲，對通膨構成上⾏⾵險、對成⻑構成下⾏⾵險；拉加德說明⽬前未
⾒第⼆輪效應的實質⾵險，且 9 ⽉以來⻑期融資成本已經上升。

### Page 2

3   拉加德宣布將於 2027 年 2 ⽉ 7 ⽇提前卸任，早於原訂 2027 年 10 ⽉任期屆滿，理由是希望能⾃由
就貨幣政策以外的議題表達意⾒。
4   決議後德國 2 年期公債殖利率跌 7.5 個基點⾄ 3.03% ， 10 年期降⾄約 3.3% 、為 8 ⽉下旬以來最
低；歐元⼀度跌⾄ 2025 年 5 ⽉以來最弱，隨後收復⾄ 1.116 美元。
5   據三位知情官員，除⾮能源價格驟降， 12/17 會議幾乎確定升息 1 碼⾄ 2.75% 後暫停； ECB 內部
估算殖利率上升將使 2027 ⾄ 2028 年成⻑累計減少 1 個百分點、通膨減少 0.9 個百分點。
三⼤結論
1  ECB 以⻑期融資成本上升替代⽴即升息，但 12 ⽉升息仍是主要情境，顯⽰能源型通膨下歐元
區利率尚未⾒頂；年底前再升 1 碼取決於布蘭特、天然氣與薪資，歐股仍以低負債、⾼現⾦流與
國防基建受益者為主。
2   拉加德提前卸任與法國財政⾵險疊加，接任⼈選將影響市場對歐洲央⾏傳導保護⼯具（ TPI ）

的信⼼；法德利差仍在⾼檔，應限制法國主權債與歐元的集中曝險，不押注 ECB ⽴即轉鴿。
3   當 12 ⽉會議前歐元區通膨與天然氣價格持續⾛⾼、法德利差再擴⼤時，應維持短存續期部位
並減少歐洲週期製造股；若能源回落使 ECB 升息後暫停、德債殖利率穩定，再逐步提⾼歐股週期
部位。
事件  2
10/29 美國第三季 GDP 成⻑年率 3.4% ，創⼀年來最快
五⼤重點
1   美國經濟分析局（ BEA ） 10/29 公布第三季實質 GDP （國內⽣產⽑額）初值季增年率 3.4% ，⾼
於第⼆季 2.2% 與市場預期 3.2% ，為⼀年來最快；修正值將於 11/25 公布。
2   ⺠間消費成⻑ 3.8% ，與第⼆季持平；企業投資（不含住宅）成⻑ 12.7% ，其中資訊處理設備⼤
增 37.4% 、軟體增 9.6% ，住宅投資則減少 3.3% 。
3  BEA 說明投資增加主要來⾃⺠間庫存投資與設備⽀出，以批發與製造業為⾸；淨出⼝為扣項，
出⼝減少、進⼝增加。
4   對⺠間國內買家的實質最終銷售成⻑ 4.9% ，⾼於第⼆季 4.6% ，顯⽰剔除庫存、貿易與政府後

的內需仍在加速。

### Page 3

5   名⽬ GDP 季增年率 5.6% ，國內購買物價指數漲幅由第⼆季 5.6% 降⾄ 3.0% ；第三季核⼼ PCE 物
價季增年率 2.5% ，低於第⼆季 3.3% 。
三⼤結論
1   設備與資訊處理設備投資⼤增，證實 AI 資本⽀出仍是美國成⻑主引擎，⽀持擴張期判斷與⾼品
質 AI 供應鏈核⼼配置；但成⻑加速也延後通膨回落，聯準會暫停不等於轉向寬鬆。
2   名⽬ GDP 季增年率 5.6% ⾼於 10 年期公債殖利率約 5% ，代表名⽬成⻑仍可吸收⾼利率，符合利
率低於成⻑率的基準情境；但這層保護需由 AI 投資轉成企業收⼊與現⾦流來驗證，不能外推為⻑
債安全。
3   當第四季消費與設備投資延續、核⼼通膨不降時，應維持短天期國庫券並低配⻑天期公債；若
修正值下修且⺠間最終銷售明顯放緩，才把短券到期資⾦分段轉向⻑債。
事件  3
10/29 美國 9 ⽉ PCE 物價年增 3.6% ，核⼼ PCE 升⾄ 3.1%
五⼤重點
1  BEA 公布 9 ⽉ PCE （個⼈消費⽀出）物價指數⽉增 0.5% 、年增 3.6% ，⽉增為 3 ⽉以來最⼤；核

⼼ PCE ⽉增 0.3% 、年增 3.1% ，⾼於 8 ⽉ 3.0% ，為 5 ⽉以來最⾼，均符合預期。
2  9 ⽉名⽬消費⽀出⽉增 0.5% ，低於預期 0.6% 與 8 ⽉ 0.9% ；實質消費⽀出幾乎零成⻑，實質可⽀
配所得⽉減 0.3% ，個⼈儲蓄率 3.8% 。
3   ⽉增主要來⾃能源：商品價格⽉增 0.8% ，汽油及其他能源商品價格⼤漲 9.2% ；服務價格⽉增
0.3% 與前⽉持平，其中運輸服務上漲 2.1% 。
4   數據公布後 10 年期公債殖利率維持在 5% 附近，美元指數升⾄ 102.2 後回落⾄ 102 下⽅；⾦價跌
破每盎司 4,030 美元，⽩銀跌⾄ 52.5 美元、為 2025 年 11 ⽉以來最低。
5  BEA 同步修正 7 、 8 ⽉數據以反映勞⼯統計局最新就業資料，下⼀期將於 11/25 公布；核⼼年增
率已連續兩個⽉上升。
三⼤結論
1   核⼼ PCE 年增升⾄ 3.1% ，實質消費與實質所得同步轉弱，顯⽰能源型通膨已開始侵蝕家庭購
買⼒；這是通膨偏⾼、需求放緩的錯配訊號，股債可能同時承壓。

### Page 4

2   核⼼⽉增 0.3% 符合預期、服務價格未加速，尚不⾜以證明漲價普遍擴散，聯準會暫停仍有數
據⽀持；但核⼼年增率回到 5 ⽉以來⾼點，市場不宜把暫停外推為降息，⾼估值成⻑股仍需以現
⾦流定價。
3   當 10 ⽉物價數據中租⾦與服務價格連續加速、油價維持每桶百元以上時，應縮短存續期間並
降低⾼估值成⻑股；若實質消費與就業同步轉弱、核⼼⽉增回落，才把短券到期資⾦轉向⻑債。
事件  4
10/29 胡塞組織宣稱攻擊沙烏地阿美設施與兩座煉油廠
五⼤重點
1   葉⾨胡塞組織 10/29 宣稱以彈道⾶彈與無⼈機攻擊沙烏地 6 處⽬標，包括拉斯坦努拉煉油廠、阿
美布蓋格設施、拉⽐格煉油廠及利雅得、達曼兩座機場；沙⽅未⽴即證實設施受損。
2   沙烏地主導的聯軍稱當晚攔截兩枚射向利雅得的彈道⾶彈，沙國官⽅通訊社表⽰碎⽚造成 1 ⼈
受傷與財物損失；胡塞組織則稱聯軍 24 ⼩時內對葉⾨發動 116 次空襲。
3   同⽇伊朗⾰命衛隊海軍稱 3 艘油輪在荷莫茲海峽南側未經協調的航道觸雷起⽕，波斯灣產油設

施與航道同時受到威脅。
4   宣稱發布前， WTI 12 ⽉期貨收每桶 102.00 美元、跌 0.76% ，布蘭特收 114.88 美元、跌 0.38% ，
美伊重啟談判的消息壓低油價；聲明後 WTI 盤後回升⾄ 103 美元上⽅。
5   美國能源部同⽇宣布授予合約，⾃戰略⽯油儲備交換逾 4,000 萬桶原油；道達爾能源執⾏⻑指
出原油海運成本已由每桶 2 ⾄ 3 美元升⾄約 25 美元。
三⼤結論
1   攻擊⽬標由航道延伸到沙烏地煉油與處理設施，地緣⾵險溢價短期難以消退，油價持續左右各
國央⾏升息路徑；原油宜定位為事件避險⽽⾮⻑期基準，不把每桶百元外推為全年⽔準。
2   美國動⽤戰略儲備與美伊談判消息限制了油價漲幅，供給衝擊與外交緩和並存；能源股可作短
期通膨對沖的衛星部位，若航運恢復、庫存回升應逐步獲利了結。
3   當沙烏地證實設施受損、布蘭特重回 4 年⾼點並帶動核⼼服務價格上⾏時，應提⾼能源事件避
險並縮短債券存續期間；若攻擊未造成實質減產且美伊談判推進，應降低原油與能源部位。

事件  5
10/29 亞⾺遜 AWS 營收年增 39% ，第四季財測遜於預期

### Page 5

五⼤重點
1   亞⾺遜 10/29 公布第三季營收 2,019 億美元、年增 12% ，略低於預期 2,022.6 億美元；每股盈餘
1.89 美元，低於預期 1.97 美元與去年同期 1.95 美元，去年同期含 Anthropic 投資利益 95 億美元。
2  AWS 營收 460 億美元、年增 39% ，連續第六季加速並優於預期；營業利益 281 億美元、營益率
13.9% ，均優於預期，其中 AWS 營業利益 178 億美元。
3   第三季資本⽀出 773 億美元，去年同期為 351 億美元，近 12 個⽉⾃由現⾦流轉為淨流出 424 億美
元；公司維持 2026 年資本⽀出約 2,200 億美元，執⾏⻑預告 2027 年將⼤幅增加。
4   第四季營收財測 2,400 億⾄ 2,470 億美元、營業利益 285 億⾄ 335 億美元，中位數低於市場預
估；財務⻑說明低軌衛星 Leo 服務推出晚於預期、⽀出集中第四季，股價盤後⼀度重挫近 12% 。
5   執⾏⻑賈⻄表⽰ AWS 未完成訂單達 5,780 億美元、年增三位數，預期 2027 與 2028 年 AWS 成⻑

均逾 40% ，並計劃 2027 年底前在 AWS 之外銷售⾃研 Trainium 晶⽚。
三⼤結論
1  AWS 加速與 5,780 億美元未完成訂單⽀持雲端需求，但⾃由現⾦流⼤幅淨流出，市場以盤後重
挫回應，雲端業者必須通過資本報酬與⾃由現⾦流驗證；選股應看 RPO （剩餘履約義務）能否
轉成營收。
2  2026 年資本⽀出維持約 2,200 億美元、 2027 年再⼤增，對伺服器、記憶體與先進封裝是訂單延
續訊號；但折舊與現⾦流壓⼒往下游累積， ODM 需以應收帳款與存貨週轉驗證。
3   當雲端業者資本⽀出續增⽽⾃由現⾦流連續為負、營業利益財測不如預期時，應降低雲端與⾼
估值 Nasdaq 部位；若 AWS 營益率維持並帶動⾃由現⾦流回正，可維持先進製程與先進封裝核⼼
配置。
事件  6
10/29 英特爾第四季營收財測低於預期，盤後⼀度重挫 22%
五⼤重點
1   英特爾 10/29 公布第三季營收 165 億美元、年增 21% ，⾮ GAAP ⽑利率 44.1% ，⾮ GAAP 每股盈

餘 0.52 美元，⾼於預期 0.26 美元；資料中⼼與 AI 部⾨營收 73 億美元、年增 78% 。
2   第四季營收財測 150 億⾄ 160 億美元，低於市場預估約 170.5 億美元；⾮ GAAP 每股盈餘 0.25 美
元、⽑利率 38.0% ，均低於第三季，股價盤後⼀度重挫 22% 。

### Page 6

3   執⾏⻑陳⽴武說明，記憶體短缺與零組件漲價壓抑 PC 出貨， OEM 客戶正加速調整庫存； EMIB
封裝⽤先進有機載板短缺與以⾊列廠停產，則限制伺服器晶⽚供貨。
4   財務⻑預估 2026 年 PC 出貨量年減約 12% 、下半年減逾 20% ， 2027 年再減 10% ；明年可能無法
產⽣正現⾦流，業界供給吃緊將延續⾄ 2027 年甚⾄ 2028 年。
5   客戶端運算部⾨營收 81 億美元、年減 5% ，晶圓代⼯部⾨營收 67 億美元、年增 58% ；財務⻑預
估第四季資料中⼼營收季增約 15% ， 2026 年資本⽀出維持 200 億美元。
三⼤結論
1   伺服器需求超過供給、 PC 卻因記憶體漲價萎縮，顯⽰ AI 帶動的記憶體缺貨正把成本推向下游
消費電⼦；具議價權的記憶體與先進封裝受惠，缺乏轉嫁能⼒的組裝與 PC 供應鏈承壓，符合上
游優於⼀般組裝的排序。
2   載板短缺與⼯廠停產限制出貨，代表 AI 供應鏈瓶頸已延伸到材料與封裝，議價權更集中於通過
驗證的上游；但英特爾明年現⾦流可能為負，不宜把伺服器需求直接等同於獲利。

3   當記憶體價格續漲⽽ PC 出貨與 OEM 庫存持續下修時，應降低 PC 相關 ODM 與⼀般 PCB 部位；
若記憶體供給改善使 PC 需求回穩、載板交期縮短，再評估回補。
事件  7
10/29 三星電⼦第三季營業利益 107.4 兆韓元， 2027 年 HBM 已售罄
五⼤重點
1   三星電⼦ 10/29 公布第三季營業利益 107.44 兆韓元，約為去年同期 12.2 兆韓元的 8.8 倍，為⾸家
單季營業利益破百兆韓元的科技公司；淨利 75.21 兆韓元，低於市場預估 89.06 兆韓元。
2   半導體部⾨營業利益 106.5 兆韓元，略低於預估 108.45 兆韓元；顯⽰器部⾨ 1.1 兆韓元，公司預
期第四季⾏動裝置營收將較第三季下降。
3   公司表⽰第三季 HBM （⾼頻寬記憶體） 4 營收較第⼆季增加逾兩倍， 2027 年 HBM 已售罄，明年
上半年開始⽣產 HBM4E ，晶⽚供給吃緊將延續⾄ 2028 年。
4  2026 年資本⽀出約 84.4 兆韓元，其中晶⽚部⾨ 76.8 兆韓元，並預計 2027 年⼤幅增加記憶體資

本⽀出；股東回報計畫延⾄ 1 ⽉底公布。
5   晶⽚獲利不如預期與股東回報延後，使三星股價盤中跌近 6% ， KOSPI 盤中跌 3% ⾄ 5,831.67
點，前⼀⽇曾因 SK 海⼒⼠財報⼤漲。

### Page 7

三⼤結論
1  HBM 售罄⾄ 2027 年、供給吃緊延⾄ 2028 年，延續記憶體以供給收斂轉為議價⼒的判斷，⽀持
記憶體⿓頭作為 AI 供應鏈核⼼；但單季獲利略低於預期即引發急跌，顯⽰⾼預期下波動放⼤。
2   三星擴⼤ 2027 年記憶體資本⽀出，短期鞏固⻑約能⾒度，⻑期則提⾼供給擴張與報價回落⾵
險；記憶體應分成核⼼與衛星，以 ASP （平均售價）、位元出貨與庫存天數決定加碼。
3   當 HBM 與 DRAM 報價續升、主要原廠存貨天數持平時，可維持記憶體⿓頭核⼼部位；若 2027
年擴產使報價⾛弱或⻑約延期，應先降低⾼ beta 記憶體與韓股槓桿部位。
事件  8
10/29 開拓重⼯未上調財測，股價重挫逾 9%
五⼤重點
1   開拓重⼯ 10/29 公布第三季營收 207 億美元、年增 17% ，創單季新⾼；調整後每股盈餘 7.20 美
元、年增 45% ，略⾼於預期 7.10 美元，調整後營益率 20.6% 。
2   能源與交通部⾨營收 87.8 億美元、年增 18% ，部⾨利潤年增 31% ；未完成訂單創 780 億美元新

⾼，較去年同期增約 96% ，資料中⼼發電設備為主要動能。
3   公司維持全年營收成⻑中⾄⾼⼗位數百分⽐的財測，並表⽰調整後營益率將接近⽬標區間底
部；第三季吸收約 6 億美元關稅成本，全年估約 22 億美元。
4   公司說明關稅是壓抑利潤率的主因，價格調升帶來 6.63 億美元貢獻僅部分抵銷；市場原預期創
紀錄訂單後上調財測，股價盤中⼀度重挫 12% ，為 2020 年 3 ⽉以來最⼤盤中跌幅。
5   股價收跌約 9.3% ，為道瓊指數跌幅第⼆⼤成分股，較 6 ⽉歷史⾼點下跌約 40% ，近 4 週累計下
跌逾 22% 。
三⼤結論
1   發電設備訂單倍增證實 AI 資料中⼼⽤電需求已延伸到電⼒設備，但未上調財測即遭重挫，顯⽰
市場要求訂單轉成利潤率；判斷電⼒設備應看在⼿訂單、交期與成本轉嫁能⼒，⽽⾮只看題材。
2   全年約 22 億美元關稅成本侵蝕利潤率，說明貿易政策已成 AI 基建供應鏈的實質成本；具在美
產能與定價權的業者較能吸收衝擊，標準品業者壓⼒較⼤。
3   當電⼒設備業者訂單續增但營益率因關稅與原料成本停滯時，應降低重電與電⼒設備中缺乏轉

嫁能⼒的部位；若價格調升⾜以覆蓋關稅並帶動營益率上修，可在訂單與回款確認後分批回補。

事件  9
10/28 聯電第三季每股盈餘 1.32 元，預告 2027 年平均售價年增約 10%
五⼤重點
1   聯電 10/28 公布第三季營收 742.4 億元、季增 8.0% 、年增 25.6% ；⽑利率 34.0% ，營益率
25.1% ，歸屬⺟公司淨利 164.3 億元、年增 12% ，每股盈餘 1.32 元。
2   產能利⽤率 92% ，晶圓出貨季增 7.3% ⾄逾 120 萬⽚ 12 吋約當晶圓，創新⾼； 22/28 奈⽶營收創
單季新⾼， 22 奈⽶占晶圓營收 18.3% 。
3   執⾏⻑王⽯說明各終端市場需求穩健， 8 吋與 12 吋均有價格改善空間，年度議價已近尾聲，預
估 2027 年綜合平均售價年增約 10% ，明年⾸季起回升。
4   第四季展望晶圓出貨季增 1% ⾄ 3% 、平均售價持穩、⽑利率持平，產能利⽤率降⾄ 90% 以下；
董事會另核准約 32 億美元預算，加速新加坡與台南擴產， 2026 年資本⽀出 20 億美元。
5   先進封裝與矽光⼦將在未來幾季貢獻營收，公司並轉換部分既有產能⽀應，⽬標未來三年 AI 相

關營收占⽐達 25% ；但財報後股價跌停。
三⼤結論
1   成熟製程產能利⽤率逾九成並預告 2027 年漲價約 10% ，顯⽰ AI 需求已外溢⾄成熟與特殊製
程，⽀持台灣晶圓代⼯維持核⼼配置；但漲價屬公司預估，須以明年⾸季實際報價與⽑利率驗
證。
2  32 億美元追加擴產與先進封裝、矽光⼦轉型，提⾼資本⽀出回收的檢驗⾨檻；財報後股價跌
停顯⽰市場已轉向檢驗獲利與現⾦回報，評價應看⽑利率、產能利⽤率與回款，⽽⾮題材。
3   當第四季產能利⽤率跌破 90% 後持續下滑、 2027 年價格協商結果低於 10% 時，應降低成熟製
程衛星部位；若漲價落實且先進封裝營收開始貢獻，可在台股晶圓代⼯族群中分批提⾼⽐重。
事件  10
10/28 愛德萬測試上修全年財測，營業利益預估上調⾄ 9,670 億⽇圓
五⼤重點
1   ⽇本半導體測試設備⼤廠愛德萬測試 10/28 上修 2026 財年（⾄ 2027 年 3 ⽉）財測：營收由 1.714
兆⽇圓上調⾄ 1.863 兆⽇圓，營業利益由 8,460 億⽇圓上調⾄ 9,670 億⽇圓。

### Page 9

2   全年淨利預估由 6,600 億⽇圓上調⾄ 7,415 億⽇圓，營益率預估由 49.4% 升⾄ 51.9% ；上半財年
淨利 3,680.8 億⽇圓，較去年同期 1,698.1 億⽇圓增逾⼀倍。
3   公司將上修歸因於 AI 相關測試需求強勁，上半年獲利另受銷量⼤增與⽇圓⾛弱帶動；並宣布
2027 年 3 ⽉ 24 ⽇前回購⾄多 2,800 億⽇圓庫藏股。
4   財報後股價 10/29 ⼀度⼤漲 9% 、收漲 5.87% ，帶動⽇經 225 指數上漲 1.0% ⾄ 64,595 點，連續第
三個交易⽇上漲。
5   上半年獲利部分來⾃⽇圓⾛弱，⽇本央⾏ 10/30 將公布利率決議（預期維持 1.25% ），若⽇圓轉
強，匯兌貢獻將縮⼩。
三⼤結論
1   測試設備營益率逾五成並上修財測，顯⽰ AI 投資已傳導⾄後段測試設備，符合⽇股聚焦具定價
權與海外市占之半導體設備⿓頭的看法。
2   部分獲利來⾃⽇圓⾛弱，⽇圓若因⽇銀升息轉強，匯兌貢獻將縮⼩；⽇股配置應分離⽇圓與出
⼝ beta ，以訂單與營益率⽽⾮匯率選股。

3   當 AI 晶⽚出貨與先進封裝產能續增、設備業者接單維持⾼檔時，可在⽇股中提⾼半導體設備⿓
頭⽐重；若⽇圓快速升值或客戶資本⽀出延後，應降低單純出⼝型部位。
待確認
1. 與上⼀輪⽐對結果：⽐對「全球⼗⼤財經事件｜ 2026/10/29 07:51 （台灣時間）」，本輪排除重複主題：聯準會
10/28 決議與華許記者會、加拿⼤央⾏升息、 Alphabet 財報（含 10/29 正式盤收跌 12.6% ）、微軟財報、英國預算案、
歐洲天然氣突破 100 歐元與 WTI 重回百元、美國 9 ⽉先期商品貿易逆差與批發庫存、機器⼈與⼯業機械第 232 條公告、
中國⼈⾏第三季貸款投向、渣打財報，均未重列。
2. 新事實晚於上⼀輪資料同步時間（ 10/29 07:51 ）：三星財報（台灣時間 10/29 08:50 ）、 ECB 決議（ 10/29 21:15 ）、
美國 GDP 與 PCE （ 10/29 20:30 ）、胡塞組織聲明（ 10/30 03:50 ）、亞⾺遜與英特爾財報（ 10/30 04:00 ），均為新的⼀

⼿事實。
3. 72 ⼩時內央⾏利率調整：加拿⼤央⾏ 10/28 升息已於上⼀輪單獨成件，本輪依規則不重列；埃及央⾏ 10/29 升息⾄
20% ，⾮主要經濟體，未單獨成件； ECB 、聯準會、紐⻄蘭、泰國、⾹港⾦管局均維持利率；中國⼈⾏ 7 天期逆回購
利率維持 1.40% ；⽇銀決議 10/30 中午前後公布（預期維持 1.25% ），本報告截稿時尚未公布。
4. 單⼀來源或未能直接核對官⽅原⽂的數字： GDP 各分項取⾃ BEA 新聞稿的媒體轉述； PCE 商品、能源與運輸服務
分項取⾃單⼀媒體整理；三星淨利、部⾨利益與 HBM 說法取⾃財報快訊，未讀取官⽅新聞稿；愛德萬財測數字取⾃
單⼀快訊；開拓重⼯收盤跌幅有約 7% 與 9.28% 兩種說法，本報告採道瓊成分股收盤統計 9.28% ；聯電股價跌停取⾃
單⼀媒體；聯電第三季淨利季減 61% 的原因官⽅摘要未說明。
5. 推算值：三星營業利益約為去年同期 8.8 倍，由 107.44 兆韓元除以 12.2 兆韓元推算； ECB 內部對殖利率影響的估算

與 12 ⽉升息⾄ 2.75% ，來⾃知情官員說法，⾮官⽅公布。
6. 胡塞組織宣稱的攻擊⽬標與傷亡尚未獲沙烏地官⽅證實，沙⽅僅證實攔截兩枚射向利雅得的⾶彈；亞⾺遜與英特
爾股價變動為盤後交易， 10/30 正式開盤反應待確認。

十月 21, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  網路與軟體‧美國

Netflix 
(NFLX.OQ/NFLX US)

第三成長曲線受阻 
重要訊息 
Netflix 於 10/20 美股收盤後發布 3Q26 財報並召開電話會議 。 
評論及分析 
3Q26 財報 優於 預期，但 裁員造成 4Q26 財測 獲利 低於市場共識 8.3% 。
3Q26 營 收 128.7 億美金、 YoY+1 1.8% ，營業利益 44.6 億美金、
YoY+ 37.3%，營益率 34.6% ，稀釋後 EPS 0. 7 元、YoY+ 48.3% ，優於市場
共識 6.3% 。不過，公司提出之 4Q26 財測則低於市場共識，營業利益及稅
後淨利分別低於 市場共識 9.2%、8.3% ，獲利較低來自 1.) 裁員一次性支
出，約造成 1.6 億美金費用，降低約 1.2 個百分點的營益率、 2.)廣告平台進
度落後，廣告收入下修約 3 億美金。  
電視遊戲及 垂直影片效益不彰，第三成長曲線受阻 。過去兩年公司持續投入
資源開發電視遊戲及垂直影片，對新業務寄予厚 望。不過， 本次財報卻對此
未有著墨，且先前公布裁員部門亦包括在內 ，顯示效益並未達到公司目標。
為了增加內容豐富度並維持用戶時占率，公司開始更積極向外購買內容授
權，包括 1.)與 Disney(DIS US) 達成部分地區的內容授權協議、2.)與法國電視
台 TF1 達成法國地區的內容授權協議、3.)取得 NFL 感恩節與聖誕節賽事 直播
權等。整體來說，我們認為更加積極地向外採購內容授權 ，意味著自製內容
的競爭力下降，同時也 將增加額外成本。  
投資建議 
我們維持對 Netflix 中性看法與持有評等，基於 1.)串流平台同業持續整併，
派拉蒙與 WBD 已於 10M26 完成合併，FOX 則預計於 1H27 完成收購
Roku 、2.)Youtube 、短影音與 AI 短片侵蝕時占率、 3.) 自製內容競爭力下
降，未有現象級作品推出。目標價由 75 元微調至 74 元，基於 2027 年 20
倍本益比評價而得。  
投資風險 
定價能力減弱、 串流娛樂平台競爭、用戶參與度下降。

十月 21, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 5

項目 定義 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括為滿足能源使用而生產的電力（公
用事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下 
購買之再生能源 
Primary 再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、生物質能、地熱能）之量  
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源 
再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例 
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟
化氮 (NF3) 
CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟
化氮 (NF3) 
廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內 
廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物 
總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮 
環保支出 環保支出總金額 - 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處置、處理、衛生、清理支出 
員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁
減或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 =（離開之員工/平均員工人數）*100 
- 平均員工人數 =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數）/2 
- 本財年末員工數 =上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數 
女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100 
女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100 
教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 =  8 小時工作 
每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數

美國 Netflix

十月 21, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 6

 增加持股  (OP) 對個股持正面看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現超越凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總報
酬。 
 持有 (N) 對個股持中性看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現符合凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總報
酬。 
 降低持股  (U) 對個股持負面看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現低於凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總報
酬。 
 未評等  (NR)  凱基證券未對該個股加以評等。

中包括提供評等給投資人參考。

### Page 1

十月 19, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 1

法說會重點 摘要

曜越 
(3540 TT, NT$24.55, 未評等)

重點摘要  
1. 1H26 EPS 0.16 元，較 1H25 成長主因業外收入挹注，營收、毛利
率、營利率年減則因消費需求疲弱，且零組件漲價。  
2. 公司由零組件跨入系統，1 -3Q26 Gaming PC 營收比重 35% ，後
續新開發 AI 工作站產品， 預計 4Q26- 2027 年量體逐漸放大。  
3. 針對關鍵零組件 CPU、記憶體等有做策略性備貨，目前庫存水準約
兩個月。 
結論 
公司展望 AI 工作站將成為 2027 年主要營收成長動能，且將帶動毛利
率擴張。 市場共識預估 2026 年 EPS 年減至 0.45- 0.5 元，2027 年年增
至 1.4-1.5 元，目前股價交易於 16-17 倍 2027 年市場共識 EPS 預估。

公司簡介與 1H26 財務狀況  
 公司 2007 年上櫃，目前資本額台幣 8.45 億元，總部位於台北，設
有美國、中國、德國、澳洲子公司，串聯在地市場。 
 公司由零組件延伸至完整系統，曜越零組件產品包括機殼、電源供
應器、水冷散熱/ 風扇、賽車模擬器、 DDR 記憶體等，近年擴充至
電競 PC 系統產品，透過全球品牌與市場通路，在國際市場銷售。  
 1H26 營收年減 12%，主因消費需求疲弱，毛利率年減 4.3ppts 至
29.3% ，營利率年減 5ppts 至 0.2% ，主因零組件成本上揚使利潤
率承壓，但後續已反映價格調漲。1H26 EPS 0.16 元，較 1H25 之
0.02 元成長，因業外收益較高。 
 1-3Q26 營收年減 9%，產品組合：g aming  PC 系統 35%、電源供
應 33%、機殼 14%、散熱 13%、其他 5%。

未來展望  
 公司推出 AI 工作站產品， 瞄準地端 AI 市場，產品線 包括： 
 雙系統 AI 電競桌機， AI 桌機提供 3,352 TOPS 算力。 
 Advanced  開發者 AI 工作站 A550 ：搭載 Nvidia  RTX 5090 32GB
顯卡、 128GB DDR5 記憶體、4TB SSD ，提供 AI 算力 3,352 
TOPS，可部署於 16-24B 等級模型。  
 Performance 專業 AI 工作站 A750 ：搭載 AMD Radeon AI  Pro 
R9700 32GB 顯卡、128GB DDR5 記憶體、4TB SSD ，提供 AI 算
力 6,124 TOPS ，可部署於 30-70B 等級模型。

曜越有較多自製零組件，有利毛利率，而曜越的品牌力也有助於零
組件備料，GPU 卡則與指定供應鏈有簽約關係，在 GPU 配給制
中，獲得 GPU 供貨能力較優。 
 目前毛利率排序：水冷產品  > 系統 > 機箱 > 電源供應器。 
 公司展望 Gaming  PC 持續成長，而 AI 工作站 將成為營收成長另一
動能 ，且 AI 工作站為產品線中較高毛利率者且高於公司平均，將
有利利潤率持續改善。  
 針對 CPU、記憶體、 SSD 有做策略性備貨，目前庫存水準約 2 個
月。
